/*
 * =========================================
 * Route Not Found
 * =========================================
 */

export function notFoundHandler(request, response) {
  response.set({
    "Cache-Control": "no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate, private",

    Pragma: "no-cache",

    Expires: "0",
  });

  return response.status(404).json({
    status: "error",

    code: "ROUTE_NOT_FOUND",

    message: `Route not found: ${request.method} ${request.originalUrl}`,
  });
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Global Error Handler
 * =========================================
 */

export function globalErrorHandler(error, request, response, next) {
  /*
   * Express delegates to its default handler
   * if a response has already started.
   */

  if (response.headersSent) {
    return next(error);
  }

  const isMalformedJson =
    error?.type === "entity.parse.failed" && error instanceof SyntaxError;

  const status = isMalformedJson ? 400 : normalizeStatus(error?.status);

  const code = isMalformedJson
    ? "INVALID_JSON"
    : normalizePublicCode(error, status);

  const message = isMalformedJson
    ? "The request body contains invalid JSON."
    : getPublicMessage(error, status);

  /*
   * Log complete technical information only
   * on the server.
   */

  console.error("Unhandled API error:", {
    method: request.method,

    url: request.originalUrl,

    name: error?.name,

    code: error?.code,

    message: error?.message,

    status,

    provider: error?.provider,

    stack: process.env.NODE_ENV === "development" ? error?.stack : undefined,
  });

  response.set({
    "Cache-Control": "no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate, private",

    Pragma: "no-cache",

    Expires: "0",
  });

  const fieldErrors = normalizeFieldErrors(error?.fieldErrors);

  const validationErrors = normalizeValidationErrors(
    error?.errors || error?.validationErrors,
  );

  return response.status(status).json({
    status: "error",

    code,

    message,

    ...(Object.keys(fieldErrors).length > 0
      ? {
          fieldErrors,
        }
      : {}),

    ...(validationErrors.length > 0
      ? {
          errors: validationErrors,
        }
      : {}),

    /*
     * Development details are useful while
     * building the API but are excluded from
     * production responses.
     */

    ...(process.env.NODE_ENV === "development"
      ? {
          debug: {
            name: error?.name || "Error",

            technicalMessage: error?.message || "Unknown error",

            provider: error?.provider || null,
          },
        }
      : {}),
  });
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Status Normalization
 * =========================================
 */

function normalizeStatus(status) {
  const numericStatus = Number(status);

  if (
    Number.isInteger(numericStatus) &&
    numericStatus >= 400 &&
    numericStatus <= 599
  ) {
    return numericStatus;
  }

  return 500;
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Public Error Code
 * =========================================
 *
 * Internal database and provider codes should
 * not automatically be returned to clients.
 * Application errors are public only when
 * they also provide a publicMessage.
 */

function normalizePublicCode(error, status) {
  const code = typeof error?.code === "string" ? error.code.trim() : "";

  const isSafeCode = /^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{1,99}$/.test(code);

  if (error?.publicMessage && isSafeCode) {
    return code;
  }

  if (status === 400) {
    return "BAD_REQUEST";
  }

  if (status === 401) {
    return "AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED";
  }

  if (status === 403) {
    return "ACCESS_FORBIDDEN";
  }

  if (status === 404) {
    return "RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND";
  }

  if (status === 409) {
    return "RESOURCE_CONFLICT";
  }

  if (status === 429) {
    return "RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED";
  }

  if (status === 503) {
    return "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE";
  }

  return "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR";
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Public Error Message
 * =========================================
 */

function getPublicMessage(error, status) {
  if (typeof error?.publicMessage === "string" && error.publicMessage.trim()) {
    return error.publicMessage.trim();
  }

  if (status === 400) {
    return "The request could not be processed.";
  }

  if (status === 401) {
    return "Authentication is required.";
  }

  if (status === 403) {
    return "You do not have permission to perform this operation.";
  }

  if (status === 404) {
    return "The requested resource was not found.";
  }

  if (status === 409) {
    return "The request conflicts with the current resource state.";
  }

  if (status === 429) {
    return "Too many requests were submitted. Please try again later.";
  }

  if (status === 503) {
    return "The service is temporarily unavailable.";
  }

  return "An unexpected server error occurred.";
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Field Error Normalization
 * =========================================
 */

function normalizeFieldErrors(fieldErrors) {
  if (
    !fieldErrors ||
    typeof fieldErrors !== "object" ||
    Array.isArray(fieldErrors)
  ) {
    return {};
  }

  const normalized = {};

  for (const [fieldName, messages] of Object.entries(fieldErrors)) {
    const safeFieldName = String(fieldName).slice(0, 100);

    const messageValues = Array.isArray(messages) ? messages : [messages];

    const safeMessages = messageValues
      .filter((message) => typeof message === "string")
      .map((message) => message.trim().slice(0, 500))
      .filter(Boolean);

    if (safeMessages.length > 0) {
      normalized[safeFieldName] = safeMessages;
    }
  }

  return normalized;
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Validation Error Normalization
 * =========================================
 */

function normalizeValidationErrors(errors) {
  if (!Array.isArray(errors)) {
    return [];
  }

  return errors
    .slice(0, 50)
    .map((validationError) => {
      if (typeof validationError === "string") {
        return {
          field: "",

          code: "VALIDATION_ERROR",

          message: validationError.trim().slice(0, 500),
        };
      }

      if (!validationError || typeof validationError !== "object") {
        return null;
      }

      const message =
        typeof validationError.message === "string"
          ? validationError.message.trim().slice(0, 500)
          : "";

      if (!message) {
        return null;
      }

      return {
        field:
          typeof validationError.field === "string"
            ? validationError.field.trim().slice(0, 100)
            : "",

        code:
          typeof validationError.code === "string"
            ? validationError.code.trim().slice(0, 100)
            : "VALIDATION_ERROR",

        message,
      };
    })
    .filter(Boolean);
}

import crypto from "node:crypto";
import fs from "node:fs";

import multer from "multer";

import { getProfilePictureUserStoragePath } from "../../services/Storage/profilePictureStorageService.js";

/*
 * =========================================
 * Configuration
 * =========================================
 */

const MAX_PROFILE_PICTURE_SIZE = 20 * 1024 * 1024;

const ALLOWED_IMAGE_TYPES = new Map([
  ["image/jpeg", ".jpg"],
  ["image/jpg", ".jpg"],
  ["image/png", ".png"],
  ["image/webp", ".webp"],
  ["image/avif", ".avif"],
  ["image/gif", ".gif"],
]);

/*
 * =========================================
 * Upload Error
 * =========================================
 */

function createUploadError({
  message,
  code = "PROFILE_PICTURE_UPLOAD_FAILED",
  status = 400,
  publicMessage = "The profile picture could not be uploaded.",
}) {
  const error = new Error(message);

  error.name = "ProfilePictureUploadError";
  error.code = code;
  error.status = status;
  error.publicMessage = publicMessage;

  return error;
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Storage Configuration
 * =========================================
 */

const storage = multer.diskStorage({
  destination(request, file, callback) {
    try {
      const userId = String(request.auth?.userId || "").trim();

      if (!userId) {
        return callback(
          createUploadError({
            message:
              "Authenticated user ID is unavailable during profile-picture upload.",
            code: "PROFILE_PICTURE_UPLOAD_USER_INVALID",
            status: 401,
            publicMessage:
              "Your session could not be verified for this upload.",
          }),
        );
      }

      const userStoragePath = getProfilePictureUserStoragePath(userId);

      fs.mkdirSync(userStoragePath, {
        recursive: true,
      });

      return callback(null, userStoragePath);
    } catch (error) {
      return callback(error);
    }
  },

  filename(request, file, callback) {
    const extension = ALLOWED_IMAGE_TYPES.get(file.mimetype);

    if (!extension) {
      return callback(
        createUploadError({
          message: `Unsupported profile-picture MIME type: ${file.mimetype}`,
          code: "PROFILE_PICTURE_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED",
          status: 400,
          publicMessage:
            "The profile picture must be a JPEG, PNG, WebP, or AVIF image.",
        }),
      );
    }

    const fileName = `${crypto.randomUUID()}${extension}`;

    return callback(null, fileName);
  },
});

/*
 * =========================================
 * File Type Filter
 * =========================================
 */

function profilePictureFileFilter(request, file, callback) {
  if (!ALLOWED_IMAGE_TYPES.has(file.mimetype)) {
    return callback(
      createUploadError({
        message: `Unsupported profile-picture MIME type: ${file.mimetype}`,
        code: "PROFILE_PICTURE_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED",
        status: 400,
        publicMessage:
          "The profile picture must be a JPEG, PNG, WebP, or AVIF image.",
      }),
    );
  }

  return callback(null, true);
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Multer Upload
 * =========================================
 */

const profilePictureUpload = multer({
  storage,

  limits: {
    fileSize: MAX_PROFILE_PICTURE_SIZE,
    files: 1,
  },

  fileFilter: profilePictureFileFilter,
}).single("picture");

/*
 * =========================================
 * Upload Middleware
 * =========================================
 */

export function uploadProfilePicture(request, response, next) {
  profilePictureUpload(request, response, (error) => {
    if (!error) {
      return next();
    }

    if (error instanceof multer.MulterError) {
      if (error.code === "LIMIT_FILE_SIZE") {
        return next(
          createUploadError({
            message: "Profile picture exceeds the 20 MiB upload limit.",
            code: "PROFILE_PICTURE_TOO_LARGE",
            status: 413,
            publicMessage: "The profile picture must be 20 MB or smaller.",
          }),
        );
      }

      if (error.code === "LIMIT_FILE_COUNT") {
        return next(
          createUploadError({
            message: "More than one profile-picture file was uploaded.",
            code: "PROFILE_PICTURE_TOO_MANY_FILES",
            status: 400,
            publicMessage: "Please upload only one profile picture.",
          }),
        );
      }

      if (error.code === "LIMIT_UNEXPECTED_FILE") {
        return next(
          createUploadError({
            message: "An unexpected profile-picture upload field was received.",
            code: "PROFILE_PICTURE_UNEXPECTED_FILE",
            status: 400,
            publicMessage:
              'The profile picture must be uploaded using the "picture" field.',
          }),
        );
      }

      return next(
        createUploadError({
          message: `Multer profile-picture upload error: ${error.code}`,
          code: "PROFILE_PICTURE_UPLOAD_INVALID",
          status: 400,
          publicMessage: "The profile picture upload could not be processed.",
        }),
      );
    }

    return next(error);
  });
}

import fs from "node:fs/promises";

import { fileTypeFromFile } from "file-type";

const ALLOWED_PROFILE_PICTURE_TYPES = new Set([
  "image/jpeg",
  "image/png",
  "image/webp",
  "image/avif",
  "image/gif",
]);

function createValidationError({
  message,
  code = "PROFILE_PICTURE_VALIDATION_FAILED",
  status = 400,
  publicMessage = "The uploaded profile picture is invalid.",
}) {
  const error = new Error(message);

  error.name = "ProfilePictureValidationError";
  error.code = code;
  error.status = status;
  error.publicMessage = publicMessage;

  return error;
}

async function removeUploadedFile(filePath) {
  if (!filePath) {
    return;
  }

  try {
    await fs.unlink(filePath);
  } catch (error) {
    /*
     * ENOENT means the file is already gone.
     * Any other cleanup failure should still be surfaced.
     */
    if (error.code !== "ENOENT") {
      throw error;
    }
  }
}

export async function validateProfilePicture(request, response, next) {
  const uploadedFile = request.file;

  if (!uploadedFile?.path) {
    return next(
      createValidationError({
        message: "No uploaded profile-picture file was available.",
        code: "PROFILE_PICTURE_FILE_REQUIRED",
        publicMessage: "Please select a profile picture to upload.",
      }),
    );
  }

  try {
    const detectedType = await fileTypeFromFile(uploadedFile.path);

    if (
      !detectedType ||
      !ALLOWED_PROFILE_PICTURE_TYPES.has(detectedType.mime)
    ) {
      await removeUploadedFile(uploadedFile.path);

      return next(
        createValidationError({
          message:
            "The uploaded file does not contain a supported image signature.",
          code: "PROFILE_PICTURE_CONTENT_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED",
          publicMessage:
            "The uploaded file must be a valid JPEG, PNG, WebP, AVIF, or GIF image.",
        }),
      );
    }

    if (detectedType.mime !== uploadedFile.mimetype) {
      await removeUploadedFile(uploadedFile.path);

      return next(
        createValidationError({
          message:
            `Profile-picture MIME mismatch: declared ${uploadedFile.mimetype}, ` +
            `detected ${detectedType.mime}.`,
          code: "PROFILE_PICTURE_TYPE_MISMATCH",
          publicMessage:
            "The uploaded file type does not match its image content.",
        }),
      );
    }

    /*
     * Store the verified values for downstream code.
     * From this point forward, detectedType is more trustworthy
     * than the browser-supplied MIME type.
     */
    request.profilePictureFile = {
      ...uploadedFile,
      verifiedMimeType: detectedType.mime,
      verifiedExtension: detectedType.ext,
    };

    return next();
  } catch (error) {
    /*
     * If validation itself fails, do not leave an uploaded
     * file behind.
     */
    try {
      await removeUploadedFile(uploadedFile.path);
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      console.error("Failed to clean up profile-picture upload:", cleanupError);
    }

    return next(error);
  }
}

import crypto from "node:crypto";
import fs from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";

import multer from "multer";

import {
  getProjectMediaDirectory,
  getProjectMediaStorageRoot,
} from "../../services/Storage/projectMediaStorageService.js";

/*
 * =========================================
 * Project Media Upload Configuration
 * =========================================
 */

const MAX_IMAGE_SIZE = 20 * 1024 * 1024;
const MAX_VIDEO_SIZE = 100 * 1024 * 1024;

const ALLOWED_MEDIA_TYPES = new Map([
  ["image/jpeg", { extension: ".jpg", maxSize: MAX_IMAGE_SIZE }],
  ["image/png", { extension: ".png", maxSize: MAX_IMAGE_SIZE }],
  ["image/webp", { extension: ".webp", maxSize: MAX_IMAGE_SIZE }],
  ["image/avif", { extension: ".avif", maxSize: MAX_IMAGE_SIZE }],
  ["image/gif", { extension: ".gif", maxSize: MAX_IMAGE_SIZE }],
  ["video/mp4", { extension: ".mp4", maxSize: MAX_VIDEO_SIZE }],
  ["video/webm", { extension: ".webm", maxSize: MAX_VIDEO_SIZE }],
]);

function createUploadError({
  message,
  code = "PROJECT_MEDIA_UPLOAD_FAILED",
  status = 400,
}) {
  const error = new Error(message);

  error.name = "ProjectMediaUploadError";
  error.code = code;
  error.status = status;
  error.publicMessage = message;

  return error;
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Authorized Project Context
 * =========================================
 */

function getAuthorizedUploadContext(request) {
  const userId = String(request.auth?.userId ?? "").trim();
  const projectId = String(request.params?.projectId ?? "").trim();
  const authorizedProject = request.authorizedProject;

  if (!userId || !authorizedProject) {
    throw createUploadError({
      message: "Project media upload is not authorized.",
      code: "PROJECT_MEDIA_UPLOAD_UNAUTHORIZED",
      status: 403,
    });
  }

  if (
    !projectId ||
    String(authorizedProject.id ?? "").trim() !== projectId
  ) {
    throw createUploadError({
      message: "Authorized Project does not match the upload destination.",
      code: "PROJECT_MEDIA_UPLOAD_PROJECT_MISMATCH",
      status: 403,
    });
  }

  return { userId, projectId };
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Storage Directory Safety
 * =========================================
 */

async function ensureSafeUploadDirectory(userId, projectId) {
  const root = getProjectMediaStorageRoot();
  const destination = getProjectMediaDirectory(userId, projectId);

  await fs.mkdir(root, { recursive: true });

  const relative = path.relative(root, destination);

  if (
    !relative ||
    relative === ".." ||
    relative.startsWith(`..${path.sep}`) ||
    path.isAbsolute(relative)
  ) {
    throw new Error("Project media upload destination is invalid.");
  }

  const directories = [
    root,
    path.dirname(destination),
    destination,
  ];

  for (const directory of directories) {
    try {
      await fs.mkdir(directory);
    } catch (error) {
      if (error?.code !== "EEXIST") {
        throw error;
      }
    }

    const stats = await fs.lstat(directory);

    if (stats.isSymbolicLink() || !stats.isDirectory()) {
      throw new Error(
        "Project media upload directory is not a regular directory.",
      );
    }
  }

  const canonicalRoot = await fs.realpath(root);
  const canonicalDestination = await fs.realpath(destination);
  const canonicalRelative = path.relative(
    canonicalRoot,
    canonicalDestination,
  );

  if (
    !canonicalRelative ||
    canonicalRelative === ".." ||
    canonicalRelative.startsWith(`..${path.sep}`) ||
    path.isAbsolute(canonicalRelative)
  ) {
    throw new Error("Project media upload destination escapes storage root.");
  }

  return destination;
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Multer Storage
 * =========================================
 */

const storage = multer.diskStorage({
  destination(request, file, callback) {
    let context;

    try {
      context = getAuthorizedUploadContext(request);
    } catch (error) {
      return callback(error);
    }

    ensureSafeUploadDirectory(context.userId, context.projectId)
      .then((directory) => callback(null, directory))
      .catch((error) => callback(error));
  },

  filename(request, file, callback) {
    const configuration = ALLOWED_MEDIA_TYPES.get(file.mimetype);

    if (!configuration) {
      return callback(
        createUploadError({
          message: "Unsupported Project media type.",
          code: "PROJECT_MEDIA_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED",
        }),
      );
    }

    const filename = `${crypto.randomUUID()}${configuration.extension}`;

    return callback(null, filename);
  },
});

/*
 * =========================================
 * Media Type Filter
 * =========================================
 */

function projectMediaFileFilter(request, file, callback) {
  try {
    getAuthorizedUploadContext(request);

    if (!ALLOWED_MEDIA_TYPES.has(file.mimetype)) {
      throw createUploadError({
        message: "Unsupported Project media type.",
        code: "PROJECT_MEDIA_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED",
      });
    }

    return callback(null, true);
  } catch (error) {
    return callback(error);
  }
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Multer Middleware
 * =========================================
 */

const projectMediaUpload = multer({
  storage,
  limits: {
    fileSize: MAX_VIDEO_SIZE,
    files: 1,
  },
  fileFilter: projectMediaFileFilter,
}).single("media");

export function uploadProjectMedia(request, response, next) {
  projectMediaUpload(request, response, (error) => {
    if (!error) {
      return next();
    }

    if (error instanceof multer.MulterError) {
      if (error.code === "LIMIT_FILE_SIZE") {
        return next(
          createUploadError({
            message: "Project media exceeds the 100 MiB upload limit.",
            code: "PROJECT_MEDIA_TOO_LARGE",
            status: 413,
          }),
        );
      }

      if (
        error.code === "LIMIT_FILE_COUNT" ||
        error.code === "LIMIT_UNEXPECTED_FILE"
      ) {
        return next(
          createUploadError({
            message: "Upload exactly one file using the media field.",
            code: "PROJECT_MEDIA_INVALID_FILE_COUNT",
          }),
        );
      }

      return next(
        createUploadError({
          message: "Project media upload could not be processed.",
          code: "PROJECT_MEDIA_UPLOAD_INVALID",
        }),
      );
    }

    return next(error);
  });
}

export {
  ALLOWED_MEDIA_TYPES,
  MAX_IMAGE_SIZE,
  MAX_VIDEO_SIZE,
};

import fs from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";

import { fileTypeFromFile } from "file-type";

import {
  ALLOWED_MEDIA_TYPES,
  MAX_IMAGE_SIZE,
  MAX_VIDEO_SIZE,
} from "./projectMediaUploadMiddleware.js";

import {
  assertSafeProjectMediaDirectory,
  getProjectMediaDirectory,
} from "../../services/Storage/projectMediaStorageService.js";

function createValidationError(message, code, status = 400) {
  const error = new Error(message);

  error.name = "ProjectMediaValidationError";
  error.code = code;
  error.status = status;
  error.publicMessage = message;

  return error;
}

function getAuthorizedFilePath(request) {
  const userId = String(request.auth?.userId ?? "").trim();
  const projectId = String(request.params?.projectId ?? "").trim();
  const authorizedProjectId = String(
    request.authorizedProject?.id ?? "",
  ).trim();

  if (!userId || !projectId || authorizedProjectId !== projectId) {
    throw createValidationError(
      "Project media validation is not authorized.",
      "PROJECT_MEDIA_VALIDATION_UNAUTHORIZED",
      403,
    );
  }

  const uploadedFile = request.file;

  if (!uploadedFile?.path) {
    throw createValidationError(
      "Please select a Project media file to upload.",
      "PROJECT_MEDIA_FILE_REQUIRED",
    );
  }

  const expectedDirectory = path.resolve(
    getProjectMediaDirectory(userId, projectId),
  );

  const uploadedPath = path.resolve(uploadedFile.path);

  if (path.dirname(uploadedPath) !== expectedDirectory) {
    throw createValidationError(
      "Project media file location is invalid.",
      "PROJECT_MEDIA_INVALID_FILE_LOCATION",
      403,
    );
  }

  return uploadedPath;
}

async function removeRejectedFile(userId, projectId, filePath) {
  const expectedDirectory = path.resolve(
    getProjectMediaDirectory(userId, projectId),
  );

  const targetPath = path.resolve(filePath);

  if (path.dirname(targetPath) !== expectedDirectory) {
    throw new Error(
      "Rejected Project media file is outside its authorized directory.",
    );
  }

  try {
    await assertSafeProjectMediaDirectory(userId, projectId);

    const stats = await fs.lstat(targetPath);

    if (stats.isSymbolicLink() || !stats.isFile()) {
      throw new Error(
        "Rejected Project media target is not a regular file.",
      );
    }

    await fs.unlink(targetPath);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error?.code !== "ENOENT") {
      throw error;
    }
  }
}

export async function validateProjectMedia(request, response, next) {
  let uploadedPath;

  try {
    uploadedPath = getAuthorizedFilePath(request);

    await assertSafeProjectMediaDirectory(
      request.auth.userId,
      request.params.projectId,
    );

    const uploadedFile = request.file;

    // Reject symbolic links and non-regular files before reading content.
    const stats = await fs.lstat(uploadedPath);

    if (stats.isSymbolicLink() || !stats.isFile()) {
      throw createValidationError(
        "The uploaded media is not a regular file.",
        "PROJECT_MEDIA_INVALID_FILE",
      );
    }

    const detectedType = await fileTypeFromFile(uploadedPath);

    if (
      !detectedType ||
      !ALLOWED_MEDIA_TYPES.has(detectedType.mime)
    ) {
      throw createValidationError(
        "The uploaded file does not contain a supported media signature.",
        "PROJECT_MEDIA_CONTENT_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED",
      );
    }

    if (detectedType.mime !== uploadedFile.mimetype) {
      throw createValidationError(
        "The uploaded media type does not match its contents.",
        "PROJECT_MEDIA_TYPE_MISMATCH",
      );
    }

    const maximumSize = detectedType.mime.startsWith("image/")
      ? MAX_IMAGE_SIZE
      : MAX_VIDEO_SIZE;


    if (stats.size > maximumSize) {
      throw createValidationError(
        "The uploaded media exceeds its permitted size.",
        "PROJECT_MEDIA_TOO_LARGE",
        413,
      );
    }

    request.projectMediaFile = {
      ...uploadedFile,
      verifiedMimeType: detectedType.mime,
      verifiedExtension: detectedType.ext,
      verifiedSize: stats.size,
    };

    return next();
  } catch (error) {
    if (uploadedPath) {
      try {
        await removeRejectedFile(
          request.auth?.userId,
          request.params?.projectId,
          uploadedPath,
        );
      } catch (cleanupError) {
        console.error(
          "Failed to clean up rejected Project media:",
          cleanupError,
        );

        return next(
          createValidationError(
            "Project media cleanup failed.",
            "PROJECT_MEDIA_CLEANUP_FAILED",
            500,
          ),
        );
      }
    }

    return next(error);
  }
}

import { getAuthenticatedProject } from "../../services/Service/projectService.js";

/*
 * =========================================
 * Project Ownership Middleware
 * =========================================
 *
 * Verifies that the requested Project belongs
 * to the authenticated user.
 *
 * This middleware must run after authentication
 * and before Project media upload processing.
 */

export async function requireProjectOwnership(request, response, next) {
  try {
    const project = await getAuthenticatedProject({
      userId: request.auth?.userId,
      projectId: request.params.projectId,
    });

    request.authorizedProject = project;

    return next();
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}

import { getDatabasePool, sql } from "../config/database.js";

/*
 * =========================================
 * Repository Errors
 * =========================================
 */

function createRepositoryError({ message, code, status = 500, publicMessage }) {
  const error = new Error(message);

  error.name = "AuthRepositoryError";
  error.code = code;
  error.status = status;
  error.publicMessage = publicMessage;

  return error;
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Record Mapping
 * =========================================
 */

function mapChallenge(record) {
  if (!record) {
    return null;
  }

  return {
    id: record.id,
    userId: record.user_id,

    pendingEmail: record.pending_email,
    normalizedPendingEmail: record.normalized_pending_email,

    purpose: record.purpose,
    codeHash: record.code_hash,

    continuationTokenHash: record.continuation_token_hash,

    attemptCount: record.attempt_count,
    maximumAttempts: record.maximum_attempts,

    expiresAt: record.expires_at,
    lastSentAt: record.last_sent_at,
    verifiedAt: record.verified_at,
    consumedAt: record.consumed_at,

    requestIpHash: record.request_ip_hash,

    createdAt: record.created_at,
  };
}

function mapUser(record) {
  if (!record) {
    return null;
  }

  return {
    id: record.id,

    email: record.email,
    normalizedEmail: record.normalized_email,
    emailVerifiedAt: record.email_verified_at,

    accountStatus: record.account_status,

    failedLoginCount: record.failed_login_count,
    lockedUntil: record.locked_until,
    lastLoginAt: record.last_login_at,

    createdAt: record.created_at,
    updatedAt: record.updated_at,
  };
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Find User by Email
 * =========================================
 */

export async function findUserByNormalizedEmail(normalizedEmail) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("normalizedEmail", sql.NVarChar(320), normalizedEmail).query(`
      SELECT TOP (1)
        id,
        email,
        normalized_email,
        email_verified_at,
        account_status,
        failed_login_count,
        locked_until,
        last_login_at,
        created_at,
        updated_at
      FROM dbo.users
      WHERE normalized_email = @normalizedEmail;
    `);

  return mapUser(result.recordset[0]);
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Remove Existing Registration Challenges
 * =========================================
 */

export async function deleteOpenRegistrationChallenges(normalizedEmail) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("normalizedEmail", sql.NVarChar(320), normalizedEmail).query(`
      DELETE FROM dbo.auth_challenges
      WHERE normalized_pending_email = @normalizedEmail
        AND purpose = N'registration'
        AND consumed_at IS NULL;

      SELECT @@ROWCOUNT AS deleted_count;
    `);

  return Number(result.recordset[0]?.deleted_count || 0);
}

/*

 * Delete One Registration Challenge
 * =========================================
 *
 * This can clean up a challenge when sending
 * its email fails.
 */

export async function deleteRegistrationChallengeById(challengeId) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("challengeId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, challengeId).query(`
      DELETE FROM dbo.auth_challenges
      WHERE id = @challengeId
        AND purpose = N'registration'
        AND consumed_at IS NULL;

      SELECT @@ROWCOUNT AS deleted_count;
    `);

  return Number(result.recordset[0]?.deleted_count || 0) > 0;
}

/*
 * =========================================

 * =========================================
 */

export async function createRegistrationChallenge({
  id,
  email,
  normalizedEmail,
  codeHash,
  expiresAt,
  lastSentAt,
  maximumAttempts = 5,
  requestIpHash = null,
}) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("id", sql.UniqueIdentifier, id)
    .input("email", sql.NVarChar(320), email)
    .input("normalizedEmail", sql.NVarChar(320), normalizedEmail)
    .input("codeHash", sql.Char(64), codeHash)
    .input("expiresAt", sql.DateTime2(3), expiresAt)
    .input("lastSentAt", sql.DateTime2(3), lastSentAt)
    .input("maximumAttempts", sql.Int, maximumAttempts)
    .input("requestIpHash", sql.Char(64), requestIpHash).query(`
      INSERT INTO dbo.auth_challenges
      (
        id,
        pending_email,
        normalized_pending_email,
        purpose,
        code_hash,
        expires_at,
        last_sent_at,
        maximum_attempts,
        request_ip_hash
      )
      OUTPUT inserted.*
      VALUES
      (
        @id,
        @email,
        @normalizedEmail,
        N'registration',
        @codeHash,
        @expiresAt,
        @lastSentAt,
        @maximumAttempts,
        @requestIpHash
      );
    `);

  return mapChallenge(result.recordset[0]);
}

 * =========================================
 * Read Registration Challenge
 * =========================================
 */

export async function getRegistrationChallengeById(challengeId) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("challengeId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, challengeId).query(`
      SELECT TOP (1) *
      FROM dbo.auth_challenges
      WHERE id = @challengeId
        AND purpose = N'registration';
    `);

  return mapChallenge(result.recordset[0]);
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Increment Failed OTP Attempt
 * =========================================
 */

export async function incrementRegistrationChallengeAttempt(challengeId) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("challengeId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, challengeId).query(`
      UPDATE dbo.auth_challenges
      SET attempt_count = attempt_count + 1
      OUTPUT
        inserted.attempt_count,
        inserted.maximum_attempts
      WHERE id = @challengeId
        AND purpose = N'registration'
        AND verified_at IS NULL
        AND consumed_at IS NULL
        AND expires_at > SYSUTCDATETIME()
        AND attempt_count < maximum_attempts;
    `);

  const record = result.recordset[0];

  if (!record) {
    return null;
  }

  return {
    attemptCount: record.attempt_count,
    maximumAttempts: record.maximum_attempts,
  };
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Mark Registration OTP Verified
 * =========================================
 */

export async function markRegistrationChallengeVerified({
  challengeId,
  continuationTokenHash,
  continuationExpiresAt,
}) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("challengeId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, challengeId)
    .input("continuationTokenHash", sql.Char(64), continuationTokenHash)
    .input("continuationExpiresAt", sql.DateTime2(3), continuationExpiresAt)
    .query(`
      UPDATE dbo.auth_challenges
      SET
        verified_at = SYSUTCDATETIME(),
        continuation_token_hash = @continuationTokenHash,
        expires_at = @continuationExpiresAt
      OUTPUT inserted.*
      WHERE id = @challengeId
        AND purpose = N'registration'
        AND verified_at IS NULL
        AND consumed_at IS NULL
        AND expires_at > SYSUTCDATETIME()
        AND attempt_count < maximum_attempts;
    `);

  return mapChallenge(result.recordset[0]);
}

export async function createLoginAuditEvent({
  userId = null,
  emailAttempted = null,
  eventType,
  wasSuccessful,
  failureReason = null,
  ipAddressHash = null,
  userAgent = null,
}) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
    .input("emailAttempted", sql.NVarChar(320), emailAttempted)
    .input("eventType", sql.NVarChar(50), eventType)
    .input("wasSuccessful", sql.Bit, Boolean(wasSuccessful))
    .input("failureReason", sql.NVarChar(100), failureReason)
    .input("ipAddressHash", sql.Char(64), ipAddressHash)
    .input("userAgent", sql.NVarChar(1000), userAgent).query(`
      INSERT INTO dbo.login_audit_events
      (
        user_id,
        email_attempted,
        event_type,
        was_successful,
        failure_reason,
        ip_address_hash,
        user_agent
      )
      OUTPUT
        inserted.id,
        inserted.created_at
      VALUES
      (
        @userId,
        @emailAttempted,
        @eventType,
        @wasSuccessful,
        @failureReason,
        @ipAddressHash,
        @userAgent
      );
    `);

  const record = result.recordset[0];

  return {
    id: record.id,
    createdAt: record.created_at,
  };
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Complete Registration
 * =========================================
 *
 * The following operations occur in one
 * serializable transaction:
 *
 * 1. Lock and verify the challenge
 * 2. Ensure the email is not registered
 * 3. Create the user
 * 4. Create the default profile
 * 5. Create the default settings
 * 6. Assign the standard user role
 * 7. Consume the registration challenge
 * 8. Write the completion audit event
 */

export async function completeRegistration({
  challengeId,
  continuationTokenHash,
  passwordHash,
}) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const transaction = new sql.Transaction(pool);

  await transaction.begin(sql.ISOLATION_LEVEL.SERIALIZABLE);

  try {
    /*
     * Lock Registration Challenge
     */

    const challengeResult = await new sql.Request(transaction)
      .input("challengeId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, challengeId)
      .input("continuationTokenHash", sql.Char(64), continuationTokenHash)
      .query(`
        SELECT TOP (1) *
        FROM dbo.auth_challenges WITH (UPDLOCK, HOLDLOCK)
        WHERE id = @challengeId
          AND purpose = N'registration'
          AND continuation_token_hash = @continuationTokenHash
          AND verified_at IS NOT NULL
          AND consumed_at IS NULL
          AND expires_at > SYSUTCDATETIME();
      `);

    const challenge = challengeResult.recordset[0];

    if (!challenge) {
      throw createRepositoryError({
        message: "The verified registration challenge is unavailable.",

        code: "REGISTRATION_CONTINUATION_INVALID",

        status: 400,

        publicMessage:
          "The registration session is invalid or expired. Start registration again.",
      });
    }

    /*
     * Prevent Duplicate Account
     */

    const existingUserResult = await new sql.Request(transaction).input(
      "normalizedEmail",
      sql.NVarChar(320),
      challenge.normalized_pending_email,
    ).query(`
        SELECT TOP (1) id
        FROM dbo.users WITH (UPDLOCK, HOLDLOCK)
        WHERE normalized_email = @normalizedEmail;
      `);

    if (existingUserResult.recordset[0]) {
      throw createRepositoryError({
        message: "A user already exists for the registration email.",

        code: "ACCOUNT_ALREADY_EXISTS",

        status: 409,

        publicMessage:
          "This registration cannot be completed. Try signing in or resetting your password.",
      });
    }

    /*
     * Create User
     */

    const userResult = await new sql.Request(transaction)
      .input("email", sql.NVarChar(320), challenge.pending_email)
      .input(
        "normalizedEmail",
        sql.NVarChar(320),
        challenge.normalized_pending_email,
      )
      .input("passwordHash", sql.NVarChar(500), passwordHash).query(`
        INSERT INTO dbo.users
        (
          email,
          normalized_email,
          password_hash,
          email_verified_at,
          account_status
        )
        OUTPUT
          inserted.id,
          inserted.email,
          inserted.normalized_email,
          inserted.email_verified_at,
          inserted.account_status,
          inserted.failed_login_count,
          inserted.locked_until,
          inserted.last_login_at,
          inserted.created_at,
          inserted.updated_at
        VALUES
        (
          @email,
          @normalizedEmail,
          @passwordHash,
          SYSUTCDATETIME(),
          N'active'
        );
      `);

    const user = userResult.recordset[0];

    /*
     * Create Profile and Settings
     */

    await new sql.Request(transaction).input(
      "userId",
      sql.UniqueIdentifier,
      user.id,
    ).query(`
        INSERT INTO dbo.user_profiles (user_id)
        VALUES (@userId);

        INSERT INTO dbo.user_settings (user_id)
        VALUES (@userId);
      `);

    /*
     * Find Standard User Role
     */

    const roleResult = await new sql.Request(transaction).query(`
      SELECT TOP (1) id
      FROM dbo.roles
      WHERE name = N'user';
    `);

    const role = roleResult.recordset[0];

    if (!role) {
      throw createRepositoryError({
        message: "The standard user role does not exist.",

        code: "STANDARD_USER_ROLE_MISSING",

        publicMessage: "The account could not be created.",
      });
    }

    /*
     * Assign Role, Consume Challenge and Audit
     */

    await new sql.Request(transaction)
      .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, user.id)
      .input("roleId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, role.id)
      .input("challengeId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, challengeId).query(`
        INSERT INTO dbo.user_roles
        (
          user_id,
          role_id
        )
        VALUES
        (
          @userId,
          @roleId
        );

        UPDATE dbo.auth_challenges
        SET consumed_at = SYSUTCDATETIME()
        WHERE id = @challengeId;

        INSERT INTO dbo.login_audit_events
        (
          user_id,
          email_attempted,
          event_type,
          was_successful,
          ip_address_hash
        )
        SELECT
          @userId,
          pending_email,
          N'registration_completed',
          1,
          request_ip_hash
        FROM dbo.auth_challenges
        WHERE id = @challengeId;
      `);

    await transaction.commit();

    return {
      user: mapUser(user),
      roles: ["user"],
    };
  } catch (error) {
    /*
     * Roll Back Every Registration Change
     */

    try {
      await transaction.rollback();
    } catch (rollbackError) {
      console.error("Registration transaction rollback failed:", rollbackError);
    }

    /*
     * Normalize Unique Constraint Errors
     */

    if (error.number === 2601 || error.number === 2627) {
      throw createRepositoryError({
        message: "A unique account value already exists.",

        code: "ACCOUNT_ALREADY_EXISTS",

        status: 409,

        publicMessage:
          "This registration cannot be completed. Try signing in or resetting your password.",
      });
    }

    throw error;
  }
}

import { getDatabasePool, sql } from "../config/database.js";

/*
 * =========================================
 * Record Mapping
 * =========================================
 */

function mapContactRequest(record) {
  if (!record) {
    return null;
  }

  return {
    id: record.id,

    userId: record.user_id,

    name: record.sender_name,

    email: record.sender_email,

    category: record.category,

    subject: record.subject,

    status: record.request_status,

    createdAt: record.created_at,
  };
}

import { getDatabasePool, sql } from "../config/database.js";

/*
 * =========================================
 * Repository Error
 * =========================================
 */

function createLoginRepositoryError({
  message,
  code,
  status = 500,
  publicMessage,
}) {
  const error = new Error(message);

  error.name = "LoginRepositoryError";

  error.code = code;
  error.status = status;
  error.publicMessage = publicMessage;

  return error;
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Record Mapping
 * =========================================
 */

function mapUser(record) {
  if (!record) {
    return null;
  }

  return {
    id: record.id,

    email: record.email,

    normalizedEmail: record.normalized_email,

    passwordHash: record.password_hash,

    emailVerifiedAt: record.email_verified_at,

    accountStatus: record.account_status,

    failedLoginCount: record.failed_login_count,

    lockedUntil: record.locked_until,

    lastLoginAt: record.last_login_at,

    createdAt: record.created_at,

    updatedAt: record.updated_at,
  };
}

function mapChallenge(record) {
  if (!record) {
    return null;
  }

  return {
    id: record.id,

    userId: record.user_id,

    pendingEmail: record.pending_email,

    normalizedPendingEmail: record.normalized_pending_email,

    purpose: record.purpose,

    codeHash: record.code_hash,

    attemptCount: record.attempt_count,

    maximumAttempts: record.maximum_attempts,

    expiresAt: record.expires_at,

    lastSentAt: record.last_sent_at,

    verifiedAt: record.verified_at,

    consumedAt: record.consumed_at,

    requestIpHash: record.request_ip_hash,

    createdAt: record.created_at,
  };
}

function mapSession(record) {
  if (!record) {
    return null;
  }

  return {
    id: record.id,

    userId: record.user_id,

    refreshTokenHash: record.refresh_token_hash,

    deviceName: record.device_name,

    userAgent: record.user_agent,

    ipAddressHash: record.ip_address_hash,

    createdAt: record.created_at,

    lastUsedAt: record.last_used_at,

    expiresAt: record.expires_at,

    revokedAt: record.revoked_at,

    revocationReason: record.revocation_reason,
  };
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Find User for Password Login
 * =========================================
 *
 * passwordHash is returned only to the login
 * service. It must never reach a controller
 * response.
 */

export async function findUserForPasswordLogin(normalizedEmail) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("normalizedEmail", sql.NVarChar(320), normalizedEmail).query(`
      SELECT TOP (1)
        id,
        email,
        normalized_email,
        password_hash,
        email_verified_at,
        account_status,
        failed_login_count,
        locked_until,
        last_login_at,
        created_at,
        updated_at
      FROM dbo.users
      WHERE normalized_email =
        @normalizedEmail;
    `);

  return mapUser(result.recordset[0]);
}

export async function recordUnknownPasswordLoginFailure({

    .request()

        event_type,

      )
      VALUES

        @failureReason,
        @ipAddressHash,
        @userAgent
      );
    `);
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Known Account Password Failure
 * =========================================
 */

export async function recordPasswordLoginFailure({
  userId,
  email,
  maximumFailures = 5,
  lockMinutes = 15,
  failureReason = "invalid_credentials",
  ipAddressHash = null,
  userAgent = null,
}) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const transaction = new sql.Transaction(pool);

  await transaction.begin(sql.ISOLATION_LEVEL.SERIALIZABLE);

  try {
    const result = await new sql.Request(transaction)
      .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
      .input("maximumFailures", sql.Int, maximumFailures)
      .input("lockMinutes", sql.Int, lockMinutes).query(`
          UPDATE dbo.users
          SET
            failed_login_count =
              failed_login_count + 1,

            locked_until =
              CASE
                WHEN failed_login_count + 1 >=
                  @maximumFailures
                THEN DATEADD(
                  MINUTE,
                  @lockMinutes,
                  SYSUTCDATETIME()
                )
                ELSE locked_until
              END,

            updated_at =
              SYSUTCDATETIME()
          OUTPUT
            inserted.failed_login_count,
            inserted.locked_until
          WHERE id = @userId;
        `);

    const updated = result.recordset[0];

    if (!updated) {
      throw createLoginRepositoryError({
        message: "The user disappeared during failed-login processing.",

        code: "LOGIN_USER_NOT_FOUND",

        status: 404,

        publicMessage: "The email or password is incorrect.",
      });
    }

    const wasLocked =
      updated.failed_login_count >= maximumFailures &&
      Boolean(updated.locked_until);

    await new sql.Request(transaction)
      .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
      .input("email", sql.NVarChar(320), email)
      .input("failureReason", sql.NVarChar(100), failureReason)
      .input("ipAddressHash", sql.Char(64), ipAddressHash)
      .input("userAgent", sql.NVarChar(1000), userAgent)
      .input("wasLocked", sql.Bit, wasLocked).query(`
        INSERT INTO dbo.login_audit_events
        (
          user_id,
          email_attempted,
          event_type,
          was_successful,
          failure_reason,
          ip_address_hash,
          user_agent
        )
        VALUES
        (
          @userId,
          @email,
          N'password_login_failed',
          0,
          @failureReason,
          @ipAddressHash,
          @userAgent
        );

        IF @wasLocked = 1
        BEGIN
          INSERT INTO dbo.login_audit_events
          (
            user_id,
            email_attempted,
            event_type,
            was_successful,
            failure_reason,
            ip_address_hash,
            user_agent
          )
          VALUES
          (
            @userId,
            @email,
            N'account_locked',
            0,
            N'maximum_password_attempts',
            @ipAddressHash,
            @userAgent
          );
        END;
      `);

    await transaction.commit();

    return {
      failedLoginCount: updated.failed_login_count,

      lockedUntil: updated.locked_until,

      wasLocked,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await transaction.rollback();
    } catch (rollbackError) {
      console.error(
        "Password-failure transaction rollback failed:",
        rollbackError,
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }
}

 * =========================================
 * Successful Password Verification

export async function recordPasswordLoginSuccess({
  userId,
  email,
  ipAddressHash = null,

    .input("userAgent", sql.NVarChar(1000), userAgent).query(`
      UPDATE dbo.users
      SET
        failed_login_count = 0,
        locked_until = NULL,
        updated_at =
          SYSUTCDATETIME()
      WHERE id = @userId;

      INSERT INTO dbo.login_audit_events
      (
        user_id,
        email_attempted,
        event_type,
        was_successful,
        failure_reason,
        ip_address_hash,
        user_agent
      )
      VALUES

        @email,
        N'password_login_succeeded',
        1,
        NULL,
        @ipAddressHash,
        @userAgent
      );
    `);
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Create Login OTP Challenge
 * =========================================
 *
 * Older incomplete login challenges for this
 * user are deleted in the same transaction.
 *
 * OTP-delivery auditing is intentionally not
 * performed here. The service records it only
 * after the email provider succeeds or fails.
 */

export async function replaceLoginChallenge({
  id,
  userId,
  email,
  normalizedEmail,
  codeHash,
  expiresAt,
  lastSentAt,
  maximumAttempts = 5,
  requestIpHash = null,
}) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const transaction = new sql.Transaction(pool);

  await transaction.begin(sql.ISOLATION_LEVEL.SERIALIZABLE);

  try {
    await new sql.Request(transaction).input(
      "userId",
      sql.UniqueIdentifier,
      userId,
    ).query(`
        DELETE FROM dbo.auth_challenges
        WHERE user_id = @userId
          AND purpose = N'login'
          AND consumed_at IS NULL;
      `);

    const result = await new sql.Request(transaction)
      .input("id", sql.UniqueIdentifier, id)
      .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
      .input("email", sql.NVarChar(320), email)
      .input("normalizedEmail", sql.NVarChar(320), normalizedEmail)
      .input("codeHash", sql.Char(64), codeHash)
      .input("expiresAt", sql.DateTime2(3), expiresAt)
      .input("lastSentAt", sql.DateTime2(3), lastSentAt)
      .input("maximumAttempts", sql.Int, maximumAttempts)
      .input("requestIpHash", sql.Char(64), requestIpHash).query(`
          INSERT INTO dbo.auth_challenges
          (
            id,
            user_id,
            pending_email,
            normalized_pending_email,
            purpose,
            code_hash,
            expires_at,
            last_sent_at,
            maximum_attempts,
            request_ip_hash
          )
          OUTPUT inserted.*
          VALUES
          (
            @id,
            @userId,
            @email,
            @normalizedEmail,
            N'login',
            @codeHash,
            @expiresAt,
            @lastSentAt,
            @maximumAttempts,
            @requestIpHash
          );
        `);

    const challenge = result.recordset[0];

    await transaction.commit();

    return mapChallenge(challenge);
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await transaction.rollback();
    } catch (rollbackError) {
      console.error(
        "Login-challenge transaction rollback failed:",
        rollbackError,
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Delete Login Challenge
 * =========================================
 *
 * Used when OTP email delivery fails.
 */

export async function deleteLoginChallengeById(challengeId) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("challengeId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, challengeId).query(`
      DELETE FROM dbo.auth_challenges
      WHERE id = @challengeId
        AND purpose = N'login'
        AND consumed_at IS NULL;

      SELECT @@ROWCOUNT AS deleted_count;
    `);

  return Number(result.recordset[0]?.deleted_count || 0) > 0;
}

 * =========================================
 * Read Login Challenge
 * =========================================
 */

export async function getLoginChallengeById(challengeId) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("challengeId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, challengeId).query(`
      SELECT TOP (1) *
      FROM dbo.auth_challenges
      WHERE id = @challengeId
        AND purpose = N'login';
    `);

  return mapChallenge(result.recordset[0]);
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Refresh Login OTP Challenge
 * =========================================
 *
 * Replaces the OTP hash and expiration while
 * preserving the challenge identifier.
 *
 * The SQL condition also enforces the resend
 * cooldown atomically, preventing concurrent
 * requests from sending multiple codes.
 */

export async function refreshLoginChallenge({
  challengeId,
  codeHash,
  expiresAt,
  lastSentAt,
  cooldownSeconds = 60,
  requestIpHash = null,
}) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("challengeId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, challengeId)
    .input("codeHash", sql.Char(64), codeHash)
    .input("expiresAt", sql.DateTime2(3), expiresAt)
    .input("lastSentAt", sql.DateTime2(3), lastSentAt)
    .input("cooldownSeconds", sql.Int, cooldownSeconds)
    .input("requestIpHash", sql.Char(64), requestIpHash).query(`
      UPDATE dbo.auth_challenges
      SET
        code_hash = @codeHash,
        attempt_count = 0,
        expires_at = @expiresAt,
        last_sent_at = @lastSentAt,
        request_ip_hash = @requestIpHash
      OUTPUT inserted.*
      WHERE id = @challengeId
        AND purpose = N'login'
        AND verified_at IS NULL
        AND consumed_at IS NULL
        AND
        (
          last_sent_at IS NULL
          OR last_sent_at <=
            DATEADD(
              SECOND,
              -@cooldownSeconds,
              SYSUTCDATETIME()
            )
        );
    `);

  return mapChallenge(result.recordset[0]);
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Failed Login OTP Attempt
 * =========================================
 */

export async function recordFailedLoginOtpAttempt({
  challengeId,
  userId,
  email,
  ipAddressHash = null,
  userAgent = null,
}) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const transaction = new sql.Transaction(pool);

  await transaction.begin(sql.ISOLATION_LEVEL.SERIALIZABLE);

  try {
    const result = await new sql.Request(transaction).input(
      "challengeId",
      sql.UniqueIdentifier,
      challengeId,
    ).query(`
          UPDATE dbo.auth_challenges
          SET
            attempt_count =
              attempt_count + 1
          OUTPUT
            inserted.attempt_count,
            inserted.maximum_attempts
          WHERE id = @challengeId
            AND purpose = N'login'
            AND verified_at IS NULL
            AND consumed_at IS NULL
            AND expires_at >
              SYSUTCDATETIME()
            AND attempt_count <
              maximum_attempts;
        `);

    const attempt = result.recordset[0];

    if (attempt) {
      await new sql.Request(transaction)
        .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
        .input("email", sql.NVarChar(320), email)
        .input("ipAddressHash", sql.Char(64), ipAddressHash)
        .input("userAgent", sql.NVarChar(1000), userAgent).query(`
          INSERT INTO dbo.login_audit_events
          (
            user_id,
            email_attempted,
            event_type,
            was_successful,
            failure_reason,
            ip_address_hash,
            user_agent
          )
          VALUES
          (
            @userId,
            @email,
            N'login_otp_failed',
            0,
            N'incorrect_otp',
            @ipAddressHash,
            @userAgent
          );
        `);
    }

    await transaction.commit();

    return attempt
      ? {
          attemptCount: attempt.attempt_count,

          maximumAttempts: attempt.maximum_attempts,
        }
      : null;
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await transaction.rollback();
    } catch (rollbackError) {
      console.error(
        "Login OTP failure transaction rollback failed:",
        rollbackError,
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Mark Login OTP Verified
 * =========================================
 */

export async function markLoginChallengeVerified({
  challengeId,
  userId,
  email,
  ipAddressHash = null,
  userAgent = null,
}) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const transaction = new sql.Transaction(pool);

  await transaction.begin(sql.ISOLATION_LEVEL.SERIALIZABLE);

  try {
    const result = await new sql.Request(transaction).input(
      "challengeId",
      sql.UniqueIdentifier,
      challengeId,
    ).query(`
          UPDATE dbo.auth_challenges
          SET
            verified_at =
              SYSUTCDATETIME()
          OUTPUT inserted.*
          WHERE id = @challengeId
            AND purpose = N'login'
            AND verified_at IS NULL
            AND consumed_at IS NULL
            AND expires_at >
              SYSUTCDATETIME()
            AND attempt_count <
              maximum_attempts;
        `);

    const challenge = result.recordset[0];

    if (challenge) {
      await new sql.Request(transaction)
        .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
        .input("email", sql.NVarChar(320), email)
        .input("ipAddressHash", sql.Char(64), ipAddressHash)
        .input("userAgent", sql.NVarChar(1000), userAgent).query(`
          INSERT INTO dbo.login_audit_events
          (
            user_id,
            email_attempted,
            event_type,
            was_successful,
            failure_reason,
            ip_address_hash,
            user_agent
          )
          VALUES
          (
            @userId,
            @email,
            N'login_otp_verified',
            1,
            NULL,
            @ipAddressHash,
            @userAgent
          );
        `);
    }

    await transaction.commit();

    return mapChallenge(challenge);
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await transaction.rollback();
    } catch (rollbackError) {
      console.error(
        "Login OTP verification transaction rollback failed:",
        rollbackError,
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Complete Login and Create Session
 * =========================================
 *
 * Challenge consumption, session insertion,
 * last-login update and login audit occur in
 * one serializable transaction.
 */

export async function completeLoginWithSession({
  challengeId,
  refreshTokenHash,
  deviceName = null,
  userAgent = null,
  ipAddressHash = null,
  expiresAt,
}) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const transaction = new sql.Transaction(pool);

  await transaction.begin(sql.ISOLATION_LEVEL.SERIALIZABLE);

  try {
    const challengeResult = await new sql.Request(transaction).input(
      "challengeId",
      sql.UniqueIdentifier,
      challengeId,
    ).query(`
          SELECT TOP (1) *
          FROM dbo.auth_challenges
            WITH (UPDLOCK, HOLDLOCK)
          WHERE id = @challengeId
            AND purpose = N'login'
            AND verified_at IS NOT NULL
            AND consumed_at IS NULL
            AND expires_at >
              SYSUTCDATETIME();
        `);

    const challenge = challengeResult.recordset[0];

    if (!challenge) {
      throw createLoginRepositoryError({
        message: "The verified login challenge is unavailable.",

        code: "LOGIN_CHALLENGE_INVALID",

        status: 400,

        publicMessage: "The login verification request is invalid or expired.",
      });
    }

    const userResult = await new sql.Request(transaction).input(
      "userId",
      sql.UniqueIdentifier,
      challenge.user_id,
    ).query(`
          SELECT TOP (1)
            id,
            email,
            normalized_email,
            password_hash,
            email_verified_at,
            account_status,
            failed_login_count,
            locked_until,
            last_login_at,
            created_at,
            updated_at
          FROM dbo.users
            WITH (UPDLOCK, HOLDLOCK)
          WHERE id = @userId
            AND account_status =
              N'active'
            AND email_verified_at
              IS NOT NULL
            AND
            (
              locked_until IS NULL
              OR locked_until <=
                SYSUTCDATETIME()
            );
        `);

    const user = userResult.recordset[0];

    if (!user) {
      throw createLoginRepositoryError({
        message: "The login account is unavailable or locked.",

        code: "LOGIN_ACCOUNT_UNAVAILABLE",

        status: 403,

        publicMessage: "This account cannot sign in at this time.",
      });
    }

    const sessionResult = await new sql.Request(transaction)
      .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, user.id)
      .input("refreshTokenHash", sql.Char(64), refreshTokenHash)
      .input("deviceName", sql.NVarChar(200), deviceName)
      .input("userAgent", sql.NVarChar(1000), userAgent)
      .input("ipAddressHash", sql.Char(64), ipAddressHash)
      .input("expiresAt", sql.DateTime2(3), expiresAt).query(`
          INSERT INTO dbo.sessions
          (
            user_id,
            refresh_token_hash,
            device_name,
            user_agent,
            ip_address_hash,
            expires_at
          )
          OUTPUT inserted.*
          VALUES
          (
            @userId,
            @refreshTokenHash,
            @deviceName,
            @userAgent,
            @ipAddressHash,
            @expiresAt
          );
        `);

    const session = sessionResult.recordset[0];

    await new sql.Request(transaction)
      .input("challengeId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, challengeId)
      .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, user.id)
      .input("email", sql.NVarChar(320), user.email)
      .input("ipAddressHash", sql.Char(64), ipAddressHash)
      .input("userAgent", sql.NVarChar(1000), userAgent).query(`
        UPDATE dbo.auth_challenges
        SET
          consumed_at =
            SYSUTCDATETIME()
        WHERE id = @challengeId;

        UPDATE dbo.users
        SET
          last_login_at =
            SYSUTCDATETIME(),

          failed_login_count = 0,

          locked_until = NULL,

          updated_at =
            SYSUTCDATETIME()
        WHERE id = @userId;

        INSERT INTO dbo.login_audit_events
        (
          user_id,
          email_attempted,
          event_type,
          was_successful,
          failure_reason,
          ip_address_hash,
          user_agent
        )
        VALUES
        (
          @userId,
          @email,
          N'login_completed',
          1,
          NULL,
          @ipAddressHash,
          @userAgent
        );
      `);

    const rolesResult = await new sql.Request(transaction).input(
      "userId",
      sql.UniqueIdentifier,
      user.id,
    ).query(`
          SELECT role.name
          FROM dbo.user_roles AS user_role
          INNER JOIN dbo.roles AS role
            ON role.id =
              user_role.role_id
          WHERE user_role.user_id =
            @userId
          ORDER BY role.name;
        `);

    await transaction.commit();

    return {
      user: mapUser(user),

      session: mapSession(session),

      roles: rolesResult.recordset.map((role) => role.name),
    };
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await transaction.rollback();
    } catch (rollbackError) {
      console.error(
        "Login-completion transaction rollback failed:",
        rollbackError,
      );
    }

    if (error.number === 2601 || error.number === 2627) {
      throw createLoginRepositoryError({
        message: "The generated session token already exists.",

        code: "SESSION_TOKEN_CONFLICT",

        status: 409,

        publicMessage:
          "The session could not be created. Please try signing in again.",
      });
    }

    throw error;
  }
}

import { getDatabasePool, sql } from "../config/database.js";

const CONTACT_CATEGORIES = [
  "emails",
  "phones",
  "websites",
  "locations",
  "socialLinks",
];

function toIso(value) {
  return value instanceof Date ? value.toISOString() : String(value || "");
}

function createEmptyCollections() {
  return {
    emails: [],
    phones: [],
    websites: [],
    locations: [],
    socialLinks: [],
  };
}

function mapProfile({ profileRow, titleRows, contactRows, pictureRows }) {
  if (!profileRow) return null;

  const contacts = createEmptyCollections();

  for (const row of contactRows) {
    if (!contacts[row.category]) continue;

    contacts[row.category].push({
      id: row.item_id,
      type: row.contact_type,
      value: row.contact_value,
      description: row.description,
      order: row.display_order,
      status: row.item_status,
      createdAt: toIso(row.created_at),
      updatedAt: toIso(row.updated_at),
    });
  }

  return {
    modelVersion: 1,
    id: profileRow.user_id,
    userId: profileRow.user_id,
    firstName: profileRow.first_name,
    middleName: profileRow.middle_name,
    lastName: profileRow.last_name,
    professionalTitles: titleRows.map((row) => ({
      id: row.item_id,
      name: row.title_name,
      description: row.description,
      order: row.display_order,
      status: row.item_status,
      createdAt: toIso(row.created_at),
      updatedAt: toIso(row.updated_at),
    })),
    ...contacts,
    profilePictures: pictureRows.map((row) => ({
      id: row.item_id,
      type: row.picture_type,
      imageUrl: row.image_url,
      storageKey: row.blob_name,
      fileName: row.file_name,
      fileType: row.file_type,
      fileSize: Number(row.file_size),
      useDefaultAvatar: Boolean(row.use_default_avatar),
      width: row.image_width === null ? null : Number(row.image_width),
      height: row.image_height === null ? null : Number(row.image_height),
      description: row.description,
      order: row.display_order,
      status: row.item_status,
      createdAt: toIso(row.created_at),
      updatedAt: toIso(row.updated_at),
    })),
    preferences: {
      primaryProfessionalTitleId:
        profileRow.primary_professional_title_id || "",
      primaryEmailId: profileRow.primary_email_id || "",
      primaryPhoneId: profileRow.primary_phone_id || "",
      primaryWebsiteId: profileRow.primary_website_id || "",
      primaryLocationId: profileRow.primary_location_id || "",
      primarySocialLinkId: profileRow.primary_social_link_id || "",
      primaryProfilePictureId: profileRow.primary_profile_picture_id || "",
    },
    visibility: {
      isPublic: profileRow.profile_visibility === "public",
    },
    status: profileRow.profile_status,
    createdAt: toIso(profileRow.created_at),
    updatedAt: toIso(profileRow.updated_at),
  };
}

async function readProfile(executor, userId) {
  const profileResult = await new sql.Request(executor).input(
    "userId",
    sql.UniqueIdentifier,
    userId,
  ).query(`
      SELECT
        user_id,
        first_name,
        middle_name,
        last_name,
        profile_visibility,
        profile_status,
        primary_professional_title_id,
        primary_email_id,
        primary_phone_id,
        primary_website_id,
        primary_location_id,
        primary_social_link_id,
        primary_profile_picture_id,
        created_at,
        updated_at
      FROM dbo.user_profiles
      WHERE user_id = @userId;
    `);

  const profileRow = profileResult.recordset[0];
  if (!profileRow) return null;

  const titleResult = await new sql.Request(executor).input(
    "userId",
    sql.UniqueIdentifier,
    userId,
  ).query(`
      SELECT item_id, title_name, description, display_order,
             item_status, created_at, updated_at
      FROM dbo.user_profile_professional_titles
      WHERE user_id = @userId
      ORDER BY display_order, created_at;
    `);

  const contactResult = await new sql.Request(executor).input(
    "userId",
    sql.UniqueIdentifier,
    userId,
  ).query(`
      SELECT item_id, category, contact_type, contact_value, description,
             display_order, item_status, created_at, updated_at
      FROM dbo.user_profile_contacts
      WHERE user_id = @userId
      ORDER BY category, display_order, created_at;
    `);

  const pictureResult = await new sql.Request(executor).input(
    "userId",
    sql.UniqueIdentifier,
    userId,
  ).query(`
      SELECT item_id, picture_type, image_url, blob_name, file_name,
             file_type, file_size, use_default_avatar, image_width,
             image_height, description, display_order, item_status,
             created_at, updated_at
      FROM dbo.user_profile_pictures
      WHERE user_id = @userId
      ORDER BY display_order, created_at;
    `);

  return mapProfile({
    profileRow,
    titleRows: titleResult.recordset,
    contactRows: contactResult.recordset,
    pictureRows: pictureResult.recordset,
  });
}

export async function findProfileByUserId(userId) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();
  return readProfile(pool, userId);
}

async function insertTitle(transaction, userId, item) {
  await new sql.Request(transaction)
    .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
    .input("itemId", sql.NVarChar(200), item.id)
    .input("name", sql.NVarChar(200), item.name)
    .input("description", sql.NVarChar(500), item.description)
    .input("displayOrder", sql.Int, item.order)
    .input("itemStatus", sql.NVarChar(20), item.status)
    .input(
      "createdAt",
      sql.DateTime2(3),
      item.createdAt ? new Date(item.createdAt) : new Date(),
    ).query(`
      INSERT INTO dbo.user_profile_professional_titles
      (user_id, item_id, title_name, description, display_order,
       item_status, created_at, updated_at)
      VALUES
      (@userId, @itemId, @name, @description, @displayOrder,
       @itemStatus, @createdAt, SYSUTCDATETIME());
    `);
}

async function insertContact(transaction, userId, category, item) {
  await new sql.Request(transaction)
    .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
    .input("itemId", sql.NVarChar(200), item.id)
    .input("category", sql.NVarChar(30), category)
    .input("contactType", sql.NVarChar(30), item.type)
    .input("contactValue", sql.NVarChar(2048), item.value)
    .input("description", sql.NVarChar(500), item.description)
    .input("displayOrder", sql.Int, item.order)
    .input("itemStatus", sql.NVarChar(20), item.status)
    .input(
      "createdAt",
      sql.DateTime2(3),
      item.createdAt ? new Date(item.createdAt) : new Date(),
    ).query(`
      INSERT INTO dbo.user_profile_contacts
      (user_id, item_id, category, contact_type, contact_value, description,
       display_order, item_status, created_at, updated_at)
      VALUES
      (@userId, @itemId, @category, @contactType, @contactValue, @description,
       @displayOrder, @itemStatus, @createdAt, SYSUTCDATETIME());
    `);
}

async function insertPicture(transaction, userId, item) {
  await new sql.Request(transaction)
    .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
    .input("itemId", sql.NVarChar(200), item.id)
    .input("pictureType", sql.NVarChar(100), item.type)
    .input("imageUrl", sql.NVarChar(2048), item.imageUrl)
    .input("storageKey", sql.NVarChar(255), item.storageKey)
    .input("fileName", sql.NVarChar(255), item.fileName)
    .input("fileType", sql.NVarChar(100), item.fileType)
    .input("fileSize", sql.BigInt, item.fileSize)
    .input("useDefaultAvatar", sql.Bit, item.useDefaultAvatar)
    .input("imageWidth", sql.Int, item.width)
    .input("imageHeight", sql.Int, item.height)
    .input("description", sql.NVarChar(500), item.description)
    .input("displayOrder", sql.Int, item.order)
    .input("itemStatus", sql.NVarChar(20), item.status)
    .input(
      "createdAt",
      sql.DateTime2(3),
      item.createdAt ? new Date(item.createdAt) : new Date(),
    ).query(`
      INSERT INTO dbo.user_profile_pictures
      (user_id, item_id, picture_type, image_url, blob_name, file_name,
       file_type, file_size, use_default_avatar, image_width, image_height,
       description, display_order, item_status, created_at, updated_at)
      VALUES
      (@userId, @itemId, @pictureType, @imageUrl, @storageKey, @fileName,
       @fileType, @fileSize, @useDefaultAvatar, @imageWidth, @imageHeight,
       @description, @displayOrder, @itemStatus, @createdAt, SYSUTCDATETIME());
    `);
}

export async function replaceProfileByUserId(userId, profile) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();
  const transaction = new sql.Transaction(pool);
  await transaction.begin(sql.ISOLATION_LEVEL.SERIALIZABLE);

  try {
    const updateResult = await new sql.Request(transaction)
      .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
      .input("firstName", sql.NVarChar(100), profile.firstName)
      .input("middleName", sql.NVarChar(100), profile.middleName)
      .input("lastName", sql.NVarChar(100), profile.lastName)
      .input(
        "visibility",
        sql.NVarChar(20),
        profile.visibility.isPublic ? "public" : "private",
      )
      .input("profileStatus", sql.NVarChar(20), profile.status)
      .input(
        "primaryTitle",
        sql.NVarChar(200),
        profile.preferences.primaryProfessionalTitleId || null,
      )
      .input(
        "primaryEmail",
        sql.NVarChar(200),
        profile.preferences.primaryEmailId || null,
      )
      .input(
        "primaryPhone",
        sql.NVarChar(200),
        profile.preferences.primaryPhoneId || null,
      )
      .input(
        "primaryWebsite",
        sql.NVarChar(200),
        profile.preferences.primaryWebsiteId || null,
      )
      .input(
        "primaryLocation",
        sql.NVarChar(200),
        profile.preferences.primaryLocationId || null,
      )
      .input(
        "primarySocial",
        sql.NVarChar(200),
        profile.preferences.primarySocialLinkId || null,
      )
      .input(
        "primaryPicture",
        sql.NVarChar(200),
        profile.preferences.primaryProfilePictureId || null,
      ).query(`
        UPDATE dbo.user_profiles
        SET first_name = @firstName,
            middle_name = @middleName,
            last_name = @lastName,
            display_name = LTRIM(RTRIM(CONCAT(@firstName, N' ', NULLIF(@middleName, N''), N' ', @lastName))),
            profile_visibility = @visibility,
            profile_status = @profileStatus,
            primary_professional_title_id = @primaryTitle,
            primary_email_id = @primaryEmail,
            primary_phone_id = @primaryPhone,
            primary_website_id = @primaryWebsite,
            primary_location_id = @primaryLocation,
            primary_social_link_id = @primarySocial,
            primary_profile_picture_id = @primaryPicture,
            updated_at = SYSUTCDATETIME()
        WHERE user_id = @userId;
      `);

    if (!updateResult.rowsAffected[0]) {
      await transaction.rollback();
      return null;
    }

    await new sql.Request(transaction).input(
      "userId",
      sql.UniqueIdentifier,
      userId,
    ).query(`
        DELETE FROM dbo.user_profile_professional_titles WHERE user_id = @userId;
        DELETE FROM dbo.user_profile_contacts WHERE user_id = @userId;
        DELETE FROM dbo.user_profile_pictures WHERE user_id = @userId;
      `);

    for (const item of profile.professionalTitles) {
      await insertTitle(transaction, userId, item);
    }

    for (const category of CONTACT_CATEGORIES) {
      for (const item of profile[category]) {
        await insertContact(transaction, userId, category, item);
      }
    }

    for (const item of profile.profilePictures) {
      await insertPicture(transaction, userId, item);
    }

    await transaction.commit();
    return findProfileByUserId(userId);
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await transaction.rollback();
    } catch (rollbackError) {
      console.error("Profile transaction rollback failed:", rollbackError);
    }

    throw error;
  }
}
