import { getDatabasePool, sql } from "../config/database.js";

const PROJECT_COLUMNS = `
  id,
  user_id,
  title,
  category,
  lifecycle_status,
  record_status,
  is_featured,
  project_data,
  created_at,
  updated_at,
  row_version
`;

function mapProjectRow(row) {
  if (!row) {
    return null;
  }

  const projectData =
    typeof row.project_data === "string"
      ? JSON.parse(row.project_data)
      : row.project_data;

  return {
    ...projectData,
    id: row.id,
    title: row.title,
    category: row.category,
    lifecycleStatus: row.lifecycle_status,
    recordStatus: row.record_status,
    presentation: {
      ...(projectData.presentation || {}),
      isFeatured: Boolean(row.is_featured),
    },
    createdAt: row.created_at,
    updatedAt: row.updated_at,
    rowVersion: Buffer.from(row.row_version).toString("base64"),
  };
}

function bindProjectParameters(request, userId, project) {
  return request
    .input("id", sql.NVarChar(200), project.id)
    .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
    .input("title", sql.NVarChar(500), project.title)
    .input("category", sql.NVarChar(100), project.category)
    .input("lifecycleStatus", sql.NVarChar(50), project.lifecycleStatus)
    .input("recordStatus", sql.NVarChar(20), project.recordStatus)
    .input("isFeatured", sql.Bit, Boolean(project.presentation?.isFeatured))
    .input("projectData", sql.NVarChar(sql.MAX), JSON.stringify(project));
}

export async function listProjectsByUser(userId) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId).query(`
      SELECT ${PROJECT_COLUMNS}
      FROM dbo.projects
      WHERE user_id = @userId
      ORDER BY updated_at DESC, id ASC;
    `);

  return result.recordset.map(mapProjectRow);
}

export async function findProjectById(userId, projectId) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
    .input("id", sql.NVarChar(200), projectId).query(`
      SELECT ${PROJECT_COLUMNS}
      FROM dbo.projects
      WHERE user_id = @userId
        AND id = @id;
    `);

  return mapProjectRow(result.recordset[0]);
}

export async function createProject(userId, project) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  await bindProjectParameters(pool.request(), userId, project).query(`
    INSERT INTO dbo.projects (
      id,
      user_id,
      title,
      category,
      lifecycle_status,
      record_status,
      is_featured,
      project_data
    )
    VALUES (
      @id,
      @userId,
      @title,
      @category,
      @lifecycleStatus,
      @recordStatus,
      @isFeatured,
      @projectData
    );
  `);

  return findProjectById(userId, project.id);
}

export async function updateProject(userId, project, expectedRowVersion) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const request = bindProjectParameters(pool.request(), userId, project).input(
    "expectedRowVersion",
    sql.Binary(8),
    Buffer.from(expectedRowVersion, "base64"),
  );

  const result = await request.query(`
    UPDATE dbo.projects
    SET
      title = @title,
      category = @category,
      lifecycle_status = @lifecycleStatus,
      record_status = @recordStatus,
      is_featured = @isFeatured,
      project_data = @projectData,
      updated_at = SYSUTCDATETIME()
    WHERE id = @id
      AND user_id = @userId
      AND row_version = @expectedRowVersion;
  `);

  if (result.rowsAffected[0] === 0) {
    return null;
  }

  return findProjectById(userId, project.id);
}

export async function deleteProject(userId, projectId, expectedRowVersion) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
    .input("id", sql.NVarChar(200), projectId)
    .input(
      "expectedRowVersion",
      sql.Binary(8),
      Buffer.from(expectedRowVersion, "base64"),
    ).query(`
      DELETE FROM dbo.projects
      WHERE id = @id
        AND user_id = @userId
        AND row_version = @expectedRowVersion;
    `);

  return result.rowsAffected[0] > 0;
}

import { getDatabasePool, sql } from "../config/database.js";

/*
 * =========================================
 * Record Mapping
 * =========================================
 */

function mapSession(record) {
  if (!record) {
    return null;
  }

  return {
    id: record.id,
    userId: record.user_id,

    refreshTokenHash: record.refresh_token_hash,

    deviceName: record.device_name,

    userAgent: record.user_agent,

    ipAddressHash: record.ip_address_hash,

    createdAt: record.created_at,

    lastUsedAt: record.last_used_at,

    expiresAt: record.expires_at,

    revokedAt: record.revoked_at,

    revocationReason: record.revocation_reason,
  };
}

function mapSessionUser(record) {
  if (!record) {
    return null;
  }

  return {
    id: record.user_id,

    email: record.email,

    normalizedEmail: record.normalized_email,

    emailVerifiedAt: record.email_verified_at,

    accountStatus: record.account_status,

    lockedUntil: record.locked_until,
  };
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Create Session
 * =========================================
 */

export async function createSession({
  userId,
  refreshTokenHash,
  deviceName = null,
  userAgent = null,
  ipAddressHash = null,
  expiresAt,
}) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
    .input("refreshTokenHash", sql.Char(64), refreshTokenHash)
    .input("deviceName", sql.NVarChar(200), deviceName)
    .input("userAgent", sql.NVarChar(1000), userAgent)
    .input("ipAddressHash", sql.Char(64), ipAddressHash)
    .input("expiresAt", sql.DateTime2(3), expiresAt).query(`
      INSERT INTO dbo.sessions
      (
        user_id,
        refresh_token_hash,
        device_name,
        user_agent,
        ip_address_hash,
        expires_at
      )
      OUTPUT inserted.*
      VALUES
      (
        @userId,
        @refreshTokenHash,
        @deviceName,
        @userAgent,
        @ipAddressHash,
        @expiresAt
      );
    `);

  return mapSession(result.recordset[0]);
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Find Active Session by Refresh Token
 * =========================================
 *
 * This lookup also confirms that the owning
 * account remains active and unlocked.
 */

export async function findActiveSessionByRefreshTokenHash(refreshTokenHash) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("refreshTokenHash", sql.Char(64), refreshTokenHash).query(`
      SELECT
        session_record.id,
        session_record.user_id,
        session_record.refresh_token_hash,
        session_record.device_name,
        session_record.user_agent,
        session_record.ip_address_hash,
        session_record.created_at,
        session_record.last_used_at,
        session_record.expires_at,
        session_record.revoked_at,
        session_record.revocation_reason,

        app_user.email,
        app_user.normalized_email,
        app_user.email_verified_at,
        app_user.account_status,
        app_user.locked_until,

        role.name AS role_name
      FROM dbo.sessions AS session_record
      INNER JOIN dbo.users AS app_user
        ON app_user.id =
          session_record.user_id
      LEFT JOIN dbo.user_roles AS user_role
        ON user_role.user_id =
          app_user.id
      LEFT JOIN dbo.roles AS role
        ON role.id =
          user_role.role_id
      WHERE session_record.refresh_token_hash =
          @refreshTokenHash
        AND session_record.revoked_at IS NULL
        AND session_record.expires_at >
          SYSUTCDATETIME()
        AND app_user.account_status =
          N'active'
        AND
        (
          app_user.locked_until IS NULL
          OR app_user.locked_until <=
            SYSUTCDATETIME()
        );
    `);

  if (result.recordset.length === 0) {
    return null;
  }

  const firstRecord = result.recordset[0];

  const roles = [
    ...new Set(
      result.recordset.map((record) => record.role_name).filter(Boolean),
    ),
  ];

  return {
    session: mapSession(firstRecord),

    user: mapSessionUser(firstRecord),

    roles,
  };
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Find Session by ID and Owner
 * =========================================
 */

export async function findSessionById({ sessionId, userId }) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("sessionId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, sessionId)
    .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId).query(`
      SELECT TOP (1) *
      FROM dbo.sessions
      WHERE id = @sessionId
        AND user_id = @userId;
    `);

  return mapSession(result.recordset[0]);
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Rotate Refresh Token
 * =========================================
 *
 * Rotation succeeds only when the supplied
 * current hash still belongs to an active,
 * unexpired session.
 *
 * If two requests attempt rotation using the
 * same token, only the first one succeeds.
 */

export async function rotateSessionRefreshToken({
  sessionId,
  currentRefreshTokenHash,
  newRefreshTokenHash,
  newExpiresAt,
  ipAddressHash = null,
  userAgent = null,
}) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("sessionId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, sessionId)
    .input("currentRefreshTokenHash", sql.Char(64), currentRefreshTokenHash)
    .input("newRefreshTokenHash", sql.Char(64), newRefreshTokenHash)
    .input("newExpiresAt", sql.DateTime2(3), newExpiresAt)
    .input("ipAddressHash", sql.Char(64), ipAddressHash)
    .input("userAgent", sql.NVarChar(1000), userAgent).query(`
      UPDATE dbo.sessions
      SET
        refresh_token_hash =
          @newRefreshTokenHash,

        last_used_at =
          SYSUTCDATETIME(),

        expires_at =
          @newExpiresAt,

        ip_address_hash =
          COALESCE(
            @ipAddressHash,
            ip_address_hash
          ),

        user_agent =
          COALESCE(
            @userAgent,
            user_agent
          )
      OUTPUT inserted.*
      WHERE id = @sessionId
        AND refresh_token_hash =
          @currentRefreshTokenHash
        AND revoked_at IS NULL
        AND expires_at >
          SYSUTCDATETIME();
    `);

  return mapSession(result.recordset[0]);
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * List Active User Sessions
 * =========================================
 */

export async function listActiveUserSessions(userId) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId).query(`
      SELECT
        id,
        user_id,
        refresh_token_hash,
        device_name,
        user_agent,
        ip_address_hash,
        created_at,
        last_used_at,
        expires_at,
        revoked_at,
        revocation_reason
      FROM dbo.sessions
      WHERE user_id = @userId
        AND revoked_at IS NULL
        AND expires_at >
          SYSUTCDATETIME()
      ORDER BY last_used_at DESC;
    `);

  /*
   * Callers receive session metadata, but
   * refresh-token hashes should not be sent
   * to the frontend.
   */

  return result.recordset.map(mapSession);
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Revoke One Owned Session
 * =========================================
 */

export async function revokeSession({
  sessionId,
  userId,
  reason = "user_revoked",
}) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("sessionId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, sessionId)
    .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
    .input("reason", sql.NVarChar(200), reason).query(`
      UPDATE dbo.sessions
      SET
        revoked_at =
          SYSUTCDATETIME(),

        revocation_reason =
          @reason
      OUTPUT inserted.*
      WHERE id = @sessionId
        AND user_id = @userId
        AND revoked_at IS NULL;
    `);

  return mapSession(result.recordset[0]);
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Revoke Session by Refresh Token
 * =========================================
 *
 * Used by logout when the browser has only
 * the refresh-token cookie.
 */

export async function revokeSessionByRefreshTokenHash({
  refreshTokenHash,
  reason = "logout",
}) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("refreshTokenHash", sql.Char(64), refreshTokenHash)
    .input("reason", sql.NVarChar(200), reason).query(`
      UPDATE dbo.sessions
      SET
        revoked_at =
          SYSUTCDATETIME(),

        revocation_reason =
          @reason
      OUTPUT inserted.*
      WHERE refresh_token_hash =
          @refreshTokenHash
        AND revoked_at IS NULL;
    `);

  return mapSession(result.recordset[0]);
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Revoke All User Sessions
 * =========================================
 *
 * exceptSessionId can preserve the browser
 * making the request.
 */

export async function revokeAllUserSessions({
  userId,
  reason = "all_sessions_revoked",
  exceptSessionId = null,
}) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("userId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, userId)
    .input("reason", sql.NVarChar(200), reason)
    .input("exceptSessionId", sql.UniqueIdentifier, exceptSessionId).query(`
      UPDATE dbo.sessions
      SET
        revoked_at =
          SYSUTCDATETIME(),

        revocation_reason =
          @reason
      WHERE user_id = @userId
        AND revoked_at IS NULL
        AND
        (
          @exceptSessionId IS NULL
          OR id <> @exceptSessionId
        );

      SELECT @@ROWCOUNT AS revoked_count;
    `);

  return Number(result.recordset[0]?.revoked_count || 0);
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Delete Expired Sessions
 * =========================================
 *
 * This is maintenance functionality for a
 * future scheduled cleanup job.
 */

export async function deleteExpiredSessions({ retentionDays = 30 } = {}) {
  const pool = await getDatabasePool();

  const result = await pool
    .request()
    .input("retentionDays", sql.Int, retentionDays).query(`
      DELETE FROM dbo.sessions
      WHERE expires_at <
        DATEADD(
          DAY,
          -@retentionDays,
          SYSUTCDATETIME()
        );

      SELECT @@ROWCOUNT AS deleted_count;
    `);

  return Number(result.recordset[0]?.deleted_count || 0);
}

import { Router } from "express";

import { rateLimit } from "express-rate-limit";

import {
  completeRegistration,
  requestRegistration,
  verifyRegistration,
} from "../controllers/registrationController.js";

import {
  logout,
  requestPasswordLogin,
  resendLoginOtp,
  verifyLogin,
} from "../controllers/loginController.js";

import {
  getCurrentSession,
  refreshSession,
} from "../controllers/sessionController.js";

/*
 * =========================================
 * Authentication Router
 * =========================================
 */

const router = Router();

/*
 * =========================================
 * Rate-Limit Response
 * =========================================
 */

function createRateLimitHandler({ code, message }) {
  return (request, response) => {
    response.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");

    return response.status(429).json({
      status: "error",

      code,

      message,
    });
  };
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Shared Rate-Limit Configuration
 * =========================================
 */

const sharedRateLimitOptions = {
  standardHeaders: "draft-8",

  legacyHeaders: false,

  /*
   * Do not count successful preflight
   * requests against authentication limits.
   */

  skip(request) {
    return request.method === "OPTIONS";
  },
};

/*
 * =========================================
 * Registration Rate Limits
 * =========================================
 */

/*
 * Limits email and OTP generation requests.
 */

const registrationRequestLimiter = rateLimit({
  ...sharedRateLimitOptions,

  windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000,

  limit: 10,

  handler: createRateLimitHandler({
    code: "REGISTRATION_REQUEST_RATE_LIMITED",

    message:
      "Too many registration requests were made. Please wait before trying again.",
  }),
});

/*
 * Allows several OTP attempts while still
 * protecting the endpoint from automation.
 *
 * The challenge itself also enforces its own
 * maximum attempt count in the database.
 */

const registrationVerificationLimiter = rateLimit({
  ...sharedRateLimitOptions,

  windowMs: 10 * 60 * 1000,

  limit: 30,

  handler: createRateLimitHandler({
    code: "REGISTRATION_VERIFICATION_RATE_LIMITED",

    message:
      "Too many verification attempts were made. Please wait before trying again.",
  }),
});

/*
 * Limits final account-creation requests.
 */

const registrationCompletionLimiter = rateLimit({
  ...sharedRateLimitOptions,

  windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000,

  limit: 10,

  handler: createRateLimitHandler({
    code: "REGISTRATION_COMPLETION_RATE_LIMITED",

    message:
      "Too many account-creation attempts were made. Please wait before trying again.",
  }),
});

/*
 * =========================================
 * Login Rate Limits
 * =========================================
 */

/*
 * Password verification is intentionally
 * restricted because password hashing is
 * computationally expensive.
 *
 * The login service and database also track
 * failures for known accounts.
 */

const passwordLoginLimiter = rateLimit({
  ...sharedRateLimitOptions,

  windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000,

  limit: 10,

  handler: createRateLimitHandler({
    code: "LOGIN_PASSWORD_RATE_LIMITED",

    message:
      "Too many sign-in attempts were made. Please wait before trying again.",
  }),
});

/*
 * The database challenge applies its own OTP
 * attempt limit. This IP-based limiter adds a
 * second layer against automated requests.
 */

const loginVerificationLimiter = rateLimit({
  ...sharedRateLimitOptions,

  windowMs: 10 * 60 * 1000,

  limit: 30,

  handler: createRateLimitHandler({
    code: "LOGIN_VERIFICATION_RATE_LIMITED",

    message:
      "Too many login verification attempts were made. Please wait before trying again.",
  }),
});

/*
 * Limits repeated OTP email delivery.
 *
 * The service also enforces a per-challenge
 * cooldown in Azure SQL.
 */

const loginResendLimiter = rateLimit({
  ...sharedRateLimitOptions,

  windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000,

  limit: 5,

  handler: createRateLimitHandler({
    code: "LOGIN_RESEND_RATE_LIMITED",

    message:
      "Too many verification codes were requested. Please wait before trying again.",
  }),
});

/*
 * =========================================
 * Session Rate Limits
 * =========================================
 */

/*
 * Refreshing a session performs a database
 * lookup and rotates its refresh token.
 */

const sessionRefreshLimiter = rateLimit({
  ...sharedRateLimitOptions,

  windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000,

  limit: 30,

  handler: createRateLimitHandler({
    code: "SESSION_REFRESH_RATE_LIMITED",

    message:
      "Too many session refresh requests were made. Please wait before trying again.",
  }),
});

/*
 * Reading the current session does not rotate
 * the token, so it can have a higher limit.
 */

const sessionReadLimiter = rateLimit({
  ...sharedRateLimitOptions,

  windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000,

  limit: 120,

  handler: createRateLimitHandler({
    code: "SESSION_READ_RATE_LIMITED",

    message:
      "Too many session requests were made. Please wait before trying again.",
  }),
});

/*
 * Logout is inexpensive, but a reasonable
 * limit prevents unnecessary database work.
 */

const logoutLimiter = rateLimit({
  ...sharedRateLimitOptions,

  windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000,

  limit: 30,

  handler: createRateLimitHandler({
    code: "LOGOUT_RATE_LIMITED",

    message:
      "Too many logout requests were made. Please wait before trying again.",
  }),
});

/*
 * =========================================
 * Registration Routes
 * =========================================
 */

router.post(
  "/register/request",

  registrationRequestLimiter,

  requestRegistration,
);

router.post(
  "/register/verify",

  registrationVerificationLimiter,

  verifyRegistration,
);

router.post(
  "/register/complete",

  registrationCompletionLimiter,

  completeRegistration,
);

/*
 * =========================================
 * Login Routes
 * =========================================
 */

router.post(
  "/login/password",

  passwordLoginLimiter,

  requestPasswordLogin,
);

router.post(
  "/login/resend",

  loginResendLimiter,

  resendLoginOtp,
);

router.post(
  "/login/verify",

  loginVerificationLimiter,

  verifyLogin,
);

/*
 * =========================================
 * Session Routes
 * =========================================
 */

router.post(
  "/session/refresh",

  sessionRefreshLimiter,

  refreshSession,
);

router.get(
  "/session",

  sessionReadLimiter,

  getCurrentSession,
);

/*
 * =========================================
 * Logout Route
 * =========================================
 */

router.post(
  "/logout",

  logoutLimiter,

  logout,
);

export default router;

import { Router } from "express";

import { rateLimit } from "express-rate-limit";

import { submitContactRequest } from "../controllers/contactController.js";

/*
 * =========================================
 * Support Router
 * =========================================
 */

const router = Router();

/*
 * =========================================
 * Contact Rate Limiter
 * =========================================
 *
 * Five submissions per IP every 15 minutes.
 */

const contactSubmissionLimiter = rateLimit({
  windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000,

  limit: 5,

  standardHeaders: "draft-8",

  legacyHeaders: false,

  skip(request) {
    if (request.method === "OPTIONS") {
      return true;
    }

    /*
     * API tests can bypass the shared in-memory
     * limiter without creating production bypass
     * behavior.
     */

    return (
      process.env.NODE_ENV === "test" &&
      request.get("x-test-bypass-rate-limit") === "true"
    );
  },

  handler(request, response) {
    response.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");

    return response.status(429).json({
      status: "error",

      code: "CONTACT_SUBMISSION_RATE_LIMITED",

      message:
        "Too many contact requests were submitted. Please wait before trying again.",
    });
  },
});

/*
 * =========================================
 * Contact Route
 * =========================================
 */

router.post(
  "/contact",

  contactSubmissionLimiter,

  submitContactRequest,
);

export default router;

import { z } from "zod";

/*
 * =========================================
 * Limits
 * =========================================
 */

export const CONTACT_NAME_MAXIMUM_LENGTH = 120;
export const CONTACT_EMAIL_MAXIMUM_LENGTH = 320;
export const CONTACT_SUBJECT_MAXIMUM_LENGTH = 160;
export const CONTACT_MESSAGE_MINIMUM_LENGTH = 20;
export const CONTACT_MESSAGE_MAXIMUM_LENGTH = 5000;

/*
 * =========================================
 * Categories
 * =========================================
 */

export const CONTACT_CATEGORIES = Object.freeze([
  "account_access",
  "profile_records",
  "ai_assistance",
  "resume",
  "portfolio",
  "privacy_security",
  "partnership",
  "feedback",
  "other",
]);

/*
 * =========================================
 * Contact Request Schema
 * =========================================
 */

export const contactRequestSchema = z
  .object({
    name: z
      .string({
        error: "Name is required.",
      })
      .trim()
      .min(1, "Name is required.")
      .max(
        CONTACT_NAME_MAXIMUM_LENGTH,
        `Name cannot contain more than ${CONTACT_NAME_MAXIMUM_LENGTH} characters.`,
      ),

    email: z
      .string({
        error: "Email is required.",
      })
      .trim()
      .min(1, "Email is required.")
      .max(
        CONTACT_EMAIL_MAXIMUM_LENGTH,
        `Email cannot contain more than ${CONTACT_EMAIL_MAXIMUM_LENGTH} characters.`,
      )
      .email("Enter a valid email address.")
      .transform((email) => email.toLowerCase()),

    category: z.enum(CONTACT_CATEGORIES, {
      error: "Select a valid request category.",
    }),

    subject: z
      .string({
        error: "Subject is required.",
      })
      .trim()
      .min(1, "Subject is required.")
      .max(
        CONTACT_SUBJECT_MAXIMUM_LENGTH,
        `Subject cannot contain more than ${CONTACT_SUBJECT_MAXIMUM_LENGTH} characters.`,
      ),

    message: z
      .string({
        error: "Message is required.",
      })
      .trim()
      .min(
        CONTACT_MESSAGE_MINIMUM_LENGTH,
        `Message must contain at least ${CONTACT_MESSAGE_MINIMUM_LENGTH} characters.`,
      )
      .max(
        CONTACT_MESSAGE_MAXIMUM_LENGTH,
        `Message cannot contain more than ${CONTACT_MESSAGE_MAXIMUM_LENGTH} characters.`,
      ),

    replyConsent: z.literal(true, {
      error: "Confirm that we may use your email address to respond.",
    }),
  })
  .strict();

/*
 * =========================================
 * Field Error Mapping
 * =========================================
 */

function mapFieldErrors(issues) {
  const fieldErrors = {};

  for (const issue of issues) {
    const fieldName = issue.path.length > 0 ? issue.path.join(".") : "_form";

    if (!fieldErrors[fieldName]) {
      fieldErrors[fieldName] = [];
    }

    fieldErrors[fieldName].push(issue.message);
  }

  return fieldErrors;
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Validation Error
 * =========================================
 */

function createContactValidationError(zodError) {
  const error = new Error("Contact request validation failed.");

  error.name = "ContactValidationError";
  error.code = "CONTACT_VALIDATION_FAILED";
  error.status = 400;
  error.publicMessage = "Review the contact information and try again.";

  error.errors = zodError.issues.map((issue) => ({
    field: issue.path.length > 0 ? issue.path.join(".") : "",
    code: issue.code,
    message: issue.message,
  }));

  error.fieldErrors = mapFieldErrors(zodError.issues);

  return error;
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Parse Contact Request
 * =========================================
 */

export function parseContactRequest(input) {
  const result = contactRequestSchema.safeParse(input);

  if (!result.success) {
    throw createContactValidationError(result.error);
  }

  return result.data;
}

FILE: D:\Documents\Project\Portfolio\portfolio-plateform\backend\src\schemas\experienceOverviewSchemas.js

import { z } from "zod";

/*
 * =========================================
 * Shared Fields
 * =========================================
 */

const shortText = z.string().trim().max(500).default("");

const longText = z.string().trim().max(5000).default("");

/*
 * =========================================
 * Context Fields
 * =========================================
 */

const responsibilitySchema = z.union([
  z.string().trim().max(1500),

  z.object({
    text: z.string().trim().max(1500).default(""),
  }),
]);

const achievementSchema = z.union([
  z.string().trim().max(1500),

  z.object({
    text: z.string().trim().max(1500).default(""),

    metric: z.string().trim().max(300).default(""),
  }),
]);

const skillSchema = z.union([
  z.string().trim().max(150),

  z.object({
    name: z.string().trim().max(150).default(""),

    nameSnapshot: z.string().trim().max(150).default(""),

    level: z.string().trim().max(50).default(""),
  }),
]);

const technologySchema = z.union([
  z.string().trim().max(150),

  z.object({
    name: z.string().trim().max(150).default(""),

    category: z.string().trim().max(100).default(""),

    proficiency: z.string().trim().max(50).default(""),

    usageDescription: z.string().trim().max(500).default(""),
  }),
]);

const leadershipSchema = z
  .object({
    hasLeadershipResponsibilities: z.boolean().default(false),

    peopleManaged: z
      .union([z.number().int().min(0).max(100000), z.null()])
      .default(null),

    description: z.string().trim().max(1500).default(""),
  })
  .default({
    hasLeadershipResponsibilities: false,
    peopleManaged: null,
    description: "",
  });

/*
 * =========================================
 * Request Schema
 * =========================================
 */

export const experienceOverviewRequestSchema = z.object({
  positionTitle: shortText,

  organizationName: shortText,

  organizationIndustry: shortText,

  experienceCategory: shortText,

  employmentType: shortText,

  workArrangement: shortText,

  responsibilities: z.array(responsibilitySchema).max(30).default([]),

  achievements: z.array(achievementSchema).max(30).default([]),

  skills: z.array(skillSchema).max(50).default([]),

  technologies: z.array(technologySchema).max(50).default([]),

  leadership: leadershipSchema,

  existingOverview: z.string().trim().max(3000).default(""),

  generationRules: z
    .object({
      numberOfSuggestions: z.number().int().min(3).max(3).default(3),

      recommendedWords: z.string().trim().max(50).default("50â€“120 words"),

      useOnlyProvidedFacts: z.boolean().default(true),

      doNotInventMetrics: z.boolean().default(true),
    })
    .default({
      numberOfSuggestions: 3,
      recommendedWords: "50â€“120 words",
      useOnlyProvidedFacts: true,
      doNotInventMetrics: true,
    }),
});

/*
 * =========================================
 * AI Response Schema
 * =========================================
 */

export const experienceOverviewResponseSchema = z.object({
  suggestions: z.array(
    z.object({
      id: z.string(),
      label: z.string(),
      text: z.string(),
    }),
  ),

  warnings: z.array(z.string()),
});

/*
 * =========================================
 * Response Normalization
 * =========================================
 */

export function normalizeExperienceOverviewResponse(value) {
  const validationResult = experienceOverviewResponseSchema.safeParse(value);

  if (!validationResult.success) {
    const error = new Error(
      "The AI provider returned an invalid experience-overview structure.",
    );

    error.status = 502;
    error.publicMessage =
      "The AI returned an invalid experience overview. Please try again.";

    error.validationIssues = validationResult.error.issues;

    throw error;
  }

  const suggestions = validationResult.data.suggestions
    .map((suggestion, index) => ({
      id: suggestion.id.trim() || `experience-overview-${index + 1}`,

      label: suggestion.label.trim() || `Option ${index + 1}`,

      text: suggestion.text.trim(),
    }))
    .filter((suggestion) => suggestion.text.length > 0)
    .slice(0, 3);

  if (suggestions.length !== 3) {
    const error = new Error(
      `Expected 3 valid experience-overview suggestions but received ${suggestions.length}.`,
    );

    error.status = 502;
    error.publicMessage =
      "The AI did not return three valid experience overviews. Please regenerate them.";

    throw error;
  }

  const warnings = validationResult.data.warnings
    .map((warning) => warning.trim())
    .filter(Boolean)
    .slice(0, 5);

  return {
    suggestions,
    warnings,
  };
}

import { z } from "zod";

/*
 * =========================================
 * Shared Values
 * =========================================
 */

const shortText = z.string().trim().max(500).default("");

const longText = z.string().trim().max(5000).default("");

const skillName = z.string().trim().min(1).max(150);

/*
 * =========================================
 * Supported Skill Values
 * =========================================
 */

export const SKILL_CATEGORY_VALUES = [
  "programming-languages",
  "software-development",
  "web-development",
  "mobile-development",
  "databases",

const responsibilitySchema = z.union([
  z.string().trim().max(1500),

  z.object({
    text: z.string().trim().max(1500).default(""),
  }),
]);

const achievementSchema = z.union([
  z.string().trim().max(1500),

  z.object({
    text: z.string().trim().max(1500).default(""),

    metric: z.string().trim().max(300).default(""),
  }),
]);

  .object({
    hasLeadershipResponsibilities: z.boolean().default(false),

    peopleManaged: z
      .union([z.number().int().min(0).max(100000), z.null()])
      .default(null),

    description: z.string().trim().max(1500).default(""),

  .default({
    hasLeadershipResponsibilities: false,
    peopleManaged: null,
    description: "",

export const experienceSkillRequestSchema = z.object({
  positionTitle: shortText,

  organizationName: shortText,

  organizationIndustry: shortText,

  experienceCategory: shortText,

  employmentType: shortText,

  workArrangement: shortText,

  overview: longText,

  responsibilities: z.array(responsibilitySchema).max(30).default([]),

  achievements: z.array(achievementSchema).max(30).default([]),

  leadership: leadershipSchema,

  existingSkills: z.array(skillName).max(100).default([]),

  technologies: z.array(skillName).max(100).default([]),

  generationRules: z
    .object({
      maximumSuggestions: z.number().int().min(3).max(12).default(8),

      excludeExistingSkills: z.boolean().default(true),

      useOnlySupportedEvidence: z.boolean().default(true),
    })
    .default({
      maximumSuggestions: 8,
      excludeExistingSkills: true,
      useOnlySupportedEvidence: true,
    }),
});

/*
 * =========================================
 * AI Response Schema
 * =========================================
 */

export const experienceSkillResponseSchema = z.object({
  suggestions: z.array(
    z.object({
      name: z.string(),
      category: z.enum(SKILL_CATEGORY_VALUES),
      type: z.enum(SKILL_TYPE_VALUES),
      reason: z.string(),
    }),
  ),

  warnings: z.array(z.string()),
});

/*
 * =========================================
 * Normalization Helpers
 * =========================================
 */

function normalizeComparisonValue(value) {
  return String(value || "")
    .normalize("NFKC")
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[._/\\-]+/g, " ")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ");
}

function normalizeWarnings(value) {
  const warnings = Array.isArray(value?.warnings) ? value.warnings : [];

  return warnings
    .map((warning) => String(warning || "").trim())
    .filter(Boolean)
    .slice(0, 5);
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Normalize AI Response
 * =========================================
 */

export function normalizeExperienceSkillResponse(value, context = {}) {
  const validationResult = experienceSkillResponseSchema.safeParse(value);

  if (!validationResult.success) {
    const error = new Error(
      "The AI provider returned an invalid experience-skill structure.",
    );

    error.status = 502;
    error.publicMessage =
      "The AI returned invalid skill suggestions. Please try again.";

    error.validationIssues = validationResult.error.issues;

    throw error;
  }

  const maximumSuggestions = context.generationRules?.maximumSuggestions || 8;

  const existingSkillNames = new Set(
    (context.existingSkills || []).map(normalizeComparisonValue),
  );

  const seenNames = new Set();

  const suggestions = validationResult.data.suggestions
    .map((suggestion) => ({
      name: suggestion.name.trim().slice(0, 150),

      category: suggestion.category,

      type: suggestion.type,

      reason: suggestion.reason.trim().slice(0, 1000),
    }))
    .filter((suggestion) => {
      const normalizedName = normalizeComparisonValue(suggestion.name);

      if (!normalizedName || !suggestion.reason) {
        return false;
      }

      if (seenNames.has(normalizedName)) {
        return false;
      }

      if (
        context.generationRules?.excludeExistingSkills !== false &&
        existingSkillNames.has(normalizedName)
      ) {
        return false;
      }

      seenNames.add(normalizedName);

      return true;
    })
    .slice(0, maximumSuggestions);

  if (suggestions.length === 0) {
    const error = new Error(
      "The AI provider did not return any usable experience-skill suggestions.",
    );

    error.status = 502;
    error.publicMessage =
      "The AI could not identify any supported skills from this experience.";

    throw error;
  }

  return {
    suggestions,
    warnings: normalizeWarnings(validationResult.data),
  };
}

import { z } from "zod";

import { MAXIMUM_PASSWORD_LENGTH } from "../security/passwordSecurity.js";

/*
 * =========================================
 * Shared Fields
 * =========================================
 */

const emailSchema = z
  .string({
    error: "Email is required.",
  })
  .trim()
  .min(1, "Email is required.")
  .max(320, "Email cannot contain more than 320 characters.")
  .email("Enter a valid email address.")
  .transform((email) => email.toLowerCase());

const passwordSchema = z
  .string({
    error: "Password is required.",
  })
  .min(1, "Password is required.")
  .max(
    MAXIMUM_PASSWORD_LENGTH,

    `Password cannot contain more than ${MAXIMUM_PASSWORD_LENGTH} characters.`,
  );

const challengeIdSchema = z
  .string({
    error: "Login challenge ID is required.",
  })
  .trim()
  .uuid("Login challenge ID must be a valid UUID.");

const otpSchema = z
  .string({
    error: "Verification code is required.",
  })
  .trim()
  .regex(
    /^\d{6}$/,

    "Verification code must contain exactly six digits.",
  );

/*
 * =========================================
 * Password Login
 * =========================================
 */

export const passwordLoginSchema = z
  .object({
    email: emailSchema,

    password: passwordSchema,
  })
  .strict();

/*
 * =========================================
 * Login OTP Verification
 * =========================================
 */

export const loginOtpVerificationSchema = z
  .object({
    challengeId: challengeIdSchema,

    otp: otpSchema,
  })
  .strict();

/*
 * =========================================
 * Login OTP Resend
 * =========================================
 */

export const loginOtpResendSchema = z
  .object({
    challengeId: challengeIdSchema,
  })
  .strict();

/*
 * =========================================
 * Field Error Mapping
 * =========================================
 */

function mapFieldErrors(issues) {
  const fieldErrors = {};

  for (const issue of issues) {
    const fieldName = issue.path.length > 0 ? issue.path.join(".") : "_form";

    if (!fieldErrors[fieldName]) {
      fieldErrors[fieldName] = [];
    }

    fieldErrors[fieldName].push(issue.message);
  }

  return fieldErrors;
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Validation Error
 * =========================================
 */

function createLoginValidationError(zodError) {
  const error = new Error("Login request validation failed.");

  error.name = "LoginValidationError";
  error.code = "LOGIN_VALIDATION_FAILED";
  error.status = 400;
  error.publicMessage = "Review the sign-in information and try again.";

  error.fieldErrors = mapFieldErrors(zodError.issues);

  error.errors = zodError.issues.map((issue) => ({
    field: issue.path.length > 0 ? issue.path.join(".") : "",

    code: issue.code,

    message: issue.message,
  }));

  return error;
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Shared Parser
 * =========================================
 */

function parseLoginInput(schema, input) {
  const result = schema.safeParse(input);

  if (!result.success) {
    throw createLoginValidationError(result.error);
  }

  return result.data;
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Parsing Helpers
 * =========================================
 */

export function parsePasswordLogin(input) {
  return parseLoginInput(
    passwordLoginSchema,

    input,
  );
}

export function parseLoginOtpVerification(input) {
  return parseLoginInput(
    loginOtpVerificationSchema,

    input,
  );
}

export function parseLoginOtpResend(input) {
  return parseLoginInput(
    loginOtpResendSchema,

    input,
  );
}

import { z } from "zod";

const itemIdSchema = z.string().trim().min(1).max(200);
const statusSchema = z.enum(["active", "archived"]);

const timestampSchema = z.string().datetime({ offset: true }).optional();

const commonItemFields = {
  id: itemIdSchema,
  description: z.string().trim().max(500).default(""),
  order: z.number().int().min(0),
  status: statusSchema,
  createdAt: timestampSchema,
  updatedAt: timestampSchema,
};

const professionalTitleSchema = z
  .object({
    ...commonItemFields,
    name: z.string().trim().min(1).max(200),
  })
  .strict();

const CONTACT_TYPES = {
  emails: ["personal", "work", "school", "business", "other"],
  phones: ["personal", "mobile", "home", "work", "school", "other"],
  websites: [
    "portfolio",
    "personal",
    "business",
    "company",
    "school",
    "blog",
    "other",
  ],
  locations: ["home", "office", "work", "school", "business", "other"],
  socialLinks: [
    "linkedin",
    "github",
    "youtube",
    "facebook",
    "instagram",
    "x",
    "other",
  ],
};

const CONTACT_LIMITS = {
  emails: 320,
  phones: 50,
  websites: 2048,
  locations: 500,
  socialLinks: 2048,
};

function createContactSchema(category) {
  return z
    .object({
      ...commonItemFields,
      type: z.enum(CONTACT_TYPES[category]),
      value: z.string().trim().min(1).max(CONTACT_LIMITS[category]),
    })
    .strict();
}

const externalUrlSchema = z
  .string()
  .trim()
  .max(2048)
  .refine(
    (value) =>
      !value ||
      (/^https:\/\//i.test(value) &&
        !/^https:\/\/(?:localhost|127\.0\.0\.1)/i.test(value)),
    "Saved picture URLs must use a public HTTPS address.",
  )
  .refine(
    (value) => !/^(?:data|blob):/i.test(value),
    "Browser-local and embedded image URLs cannot be saved.",
  );

const profilePictureSchema = z
  .object({
    ...commonItemFields,
    type: z.string().trim().max(100).default(""),
    imageUrl: externalUrlSchema.default(""),
    storageKey: z.string().trim().max(255).default(""),
    fileName: z.string().trim().max(255).default(""),
    fileType: z.string().trim().max(100).default(""),
    fileSize: z
      .number()
      .int()
      .min(0)
      .max(20 * 1024 * 1024)
      .default(0),
    useDefaultAvatar: z.boolean().default(false),
    width: z.number().int().positive().nullable().default(null),
    height: z.number().int().positive().nullable().default(null),
  })
  .strict()
  .superRefine((picture, context) => {
    if (picture.useDefaultAvatar && (picture.imageUrl || picture.storageKey)) {
      context.addIssue({
        code: "custom",
        path: ["useDefaultAvatar"],
        message: "A default avatar cannot also reference an uploaded image.",
      });
    }
  });

const preferenceSchema = z
  .object({
    primaryProfessionalTitleId: z.string().trim().max(200).default(""),
    primaryEmailId: z.string().trim().max(200).default(""),
    primaryPhoneId: z.string().trim().max(200).default(""),
    primaryWebsiteId: z.string().trim().max(200).default(""),
    primaryLocationId: z.string().trim().max(200).default(""),
    primarySocialLinkId: z.string().trim().max(200).default(""),
    primaryProfilePictureId: z.string().trim().max(200).default(""),
  })
  .strict();

const visibilitySchema = z
  .object({
    isPublic: z.boolean().default(false),
  })
  .strict();

export const profileUpdateSchema = z
  .object({
    firstName: z.string().trim().max(100).default(""),
    middleName: z.string().trim().max(100).default(""),
    lastName: z.string().trim().max(100).default(""),
    professionalTitles: z.array(professionalTitleSchema).max(20).default([]),
    emails: z.array(createContactSchema("emails")).max(20).default([]),
    phones: z.array(createContactSchema("phones")).max(20).default([]),
    websites: z.array(createContactSchema("websites")).max(20).default([]),
    locations: z.array(createContactSchema("locations")).max(20).default([]),
    socialLinks: z
      .array(createContactSchema("socialLinks"))
      .max(30)
      .default([]),
    profilePictures: z.array(profilePictureSchema).max(20).default([]),
    preferences: preferenceSchema.default({}),
    visibility: visibilitySchema.default({}),
    status: statusSchema.default("active"),
  })
  .strict()
  .superRefine((profile, context) => {
    const mappings = [
      ["primaryProfessionalTitleId", "professionalTitles"],
      ["primaryEmailId", "emails"],
      ["primaryPhoneId", "phones"],
      ["primaryWebsiteId", "websites"],
      ["primaryLocationId", "locations"],
      ["primarySocialLinkId", "socialLinks"],
      ["primaryProfilePictureId", "profilePictures"],
    ];

    for (const [preferenceName, collectionName] of mappings) {
      const requestedId = profile.preferences[preferenceName];

      if (!requestedId) continue;

      const isActiveItem = profile[collectionName].some(
        (item) => item.id === requestedId && item.status === "active",
      );

      if (!isActiveItem) {
        context.addIssue({
          code: "custom",
          path: ["preferences", preferenceName],
          message:
            "The primary item must reference an active item in its collection.",
        });
      }
    }
  });

function createValidationError(zodError) {
  const error = new Error("Profile validation failed.");
  error.name = "ProfileValidationError";
  error.code = "PROFILE_VALIDATION_FAILED";
  error.status = 400;
  error.publicMessage = "Review the Profile information and try again.";
  error.errors = zodError.issues.map((issue) => ({
    field: issue.path.join("."),
    code: issue.code,
    message: issue.message,
  }));
  error.fieldErrors = {};

  for (const issue of error.errors) {
    const field = issue.field || "_form";
    error.fieldErrors[field] ||= [];
    error.fieldErrors[field].push(issue.message);
  }

  return error;
}

export function parseProfileUpdate(input) {
  const result = profileUpdateSchema.safeParse(input);

  if (!result.success) {
    throw createValidationError(result.error);
  }

  return result.data;
}

const CATEGORY_VALUES = new Set([
  "software", "web-application", "mobile-application",
  "artificial-intelligence", "data", "cloud-infrastructure",
  "networking", "cybersecurity", "embedded-systems",
  "electronics", "automation", "internet-of-things",
  "research", "design", "business", "academic",
  "community", "other",
]);

const OPTIONS = {
  ownership: ["individual", "team", "client", "organization"],
  source: ["manual", "import", "integration", "ai-assisted"],
  proficiency: ["", "foundational", "intermediate", "advanced", "expert"],
  recognitionType: [
    "", "award", "competition", "grant", "fellowship", "showcase",
    "publication", "presentation", "patent", "media",
    "client-recognition", "academic-recognition", "other",
  ],
  placement: [
    "", "winner", "first-place", "second-place", "third-place",
    "finalist", "semifinalist", "honorable-mention", "selected",
    "recipient", "participant", "other",
  ],
  mediaType: ["image", "video", "diagram"],
  documentType: [
    "report", "presentation", "documentation", "source-file",
    "dataset", "design", "award", "supporting-evidence", "other",
  ],
  visibility: ["public", "private"],
  recordStatus: ["active", "archived"],
};

const TEXT_LIMITS = {
  "organization.name": 200,
  "organization.clientName": 200,
  "organization.website": 1000,
  "location.displayValue": 300,
  "problem.statement": 4000,
  "problem.targetAudience": 1000,
  "problem.context": 3000,
  "problem.importance": 3000,
  "solution.overview": 4000,
  "solution.approach": 4000,
  "solution.architecture": 4000,
  "results.outcome": 4000,
  "results.problemsSolved": 3000,
  "results.impact": 3000,
  "results.beneficiariesAffected": 2000,
  "results.lessonsLearned": 3000,
  "results.futureImprovements": 3000,
  "presentation.shortSummary": 500,
  "presentation.caseStudy": 12000,
  "presentation.featuredMediaId": 200,
  "privateInformation.notes": 5000,
  "sourceContext": 5000 / 10,
};

const COLLECTION_FIELDS = [
  ["problem.constraints", 1500],
  ["problem.objectives", 1500],
  ["solution.features", 1500],
  ["solution.contributions", 1500],
];

function object(value) {
  return value !== null &&
    typeof value === "object" &&
    !Array.isArray(value);
}

function fieldValue(source, path) {
  return path.split(".").reduce(
    (current, key) => object(current) ? current[key] : undefined,
    source,
  );
}

function error(errors, field, code, message) {
  errors.push({ field, code, message });
}

function checkText(errors, field, value, maximum) {
  if (value === undefined) return;

  if (typeof value !== "string" || value.trim().length > maximum) {
    error(
      errors, field, "PROJECT_FIELD_INVALID",
      `This field must be text with at most ${maximum} characters.`,
    );
  }
}

function checkOption(errors, field, value, options) {
  if (value === undefined) return;

  if (!options.includes(value)) {
    error(errors, field, "PROJECT_OPTION_INVALID", "Invalid option.");
  }
}

function checkUrl(errors, field, value, maximum = 2000) {
  if (value === undefined) return;

  checkText(errors, field, value, maximum);

  if (typeof value !== "string" || !value.trim()) return;

  try {
    const url = new URL(value);
    if (!["http:", "https:"].includes(url.protocol)) throw new Error();
  } catch {
    error(errors, field, "PROJECT_URL_INVALID", "Invalid HTTP or HTTPS URL.");
  }
}

function checkItems(errors, source, path, callback) {
  const items = fieldValue(source, path);
  if (items === undefined || !Array.isArray(items)) return;

  items.forEach((item, index) => {
    const base = `${path}.${index}`;

    if (!object(item)) {
      error(errors, base, "PROJECT_ITEM_INVALID", "Expected an object.");
      return;
    }

    callback(item, base);
  });
}

function checkOrderedItems(errors, source, path, maximum) {
  checkItems(errors, source, path, (item, base) => {
    checkText(errors, `${base}.id`, item.id, 200);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.text`, item.text, maximum);
  });
}

export function validateNestedProject(source) {
  const errors = [];

  for (const [field, maximum] of Object.entries(TEXT_LIMITS)) {
    checkText(errors, field, fieldValue(source, field), maximum);
  }

  checkOption(errors, "ownership", source.ownership, OPTIONS.ownership);
  checkOption(errors, "source", source.source, OPTIONS.source);

  if (source.modelVersion !== undefined && source.modelVersion !== 2) {
    error(errors, "modelVersion", "PROJECT_VERSION_INVALID",
      "Unsupported Project model version.");
  }

  for (const field of [
    "liveUrl", "repositoryUrl", "documentationUrl",
    "caseStudyUrl", "videoUrl",
  ]) {
    checkUrl(errors, `links.${field}`, source.links?.[field]);
  }

  checkUrl(errors, "organization.website", source.organization?.website, 1000);

  for (const [path, maximum] of COLLECTION_FIELDS) {
    checkOrderedItems(errors, source, path, maximum);
  }

  checkItems(errors, source, "results.metrics", (item, base) => {
    checkText(errors, `${base}.label`, item.label, 200);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.value`, item.value, 200);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.description`, item.description, 500);
  });

  checkItems(errors, source, "skillRelationships", (item, base) => {
    checkText(errors, `${base}.nameSnapshot`, item.nameSnapshot, 200);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.usageDescription`, item.usageDescription, 1000);
    checkOption(errors, `${base}.demonstratedProficiency`,
      item.demonstratedProficiency, OPTIONS.proficiency);

    if (!item.skillId && !item.nameSnapshot) {
      error(errors, base, "PROJECT_SKILL_IDENTITY_REQUIRED",
        "A Skill identifier or snapshot is required.");
    }
  });

  checkItems(errors, source, "technologies", (item, base) => {
    checkText(errors, `${base}.name`, item.name, 150);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.category`, item.category, 100);
  });

  const relationshipTypes = [
    ["experienceRelationships", "experienceId"],
    ["educationRelationships", "educationId"],
    ["trainingRelationships", "trainingId"],
    ["certificationRelationships", "certificationId"],
  ];

  for (const [name, idField] of relationshipTypes) {
    checkItems(errors, source, `relatedRecords.${name}`, (item, base) => {
      checkText(errors, `${base}.${idField}`, item[idField], 200);
      checkText(errors, `${base}.description`, item.description, 1000);

      if (!item[idField]) {
        error(errors, `${base}.${idField}`, "PROJECT_RELATIONSHIP_ID_REQUIRED",
          "A related record identifier is required.");
      }

      if (item.snapshot !== undefined && !object(item.snapshot)) {
        error(errors, `${base}.snapshot`, "PROJECT_SNAPSHOT_INVALID",
          "Snapshot must be an object.");
      } else {
        checkText(errors, `${base}.snapshot.name`, item.snapshot?.name, 200);
        checkText(errors, `${base}.snapshot.secondaryLabel`,
          item.snapshot?.secondaryLabel, 200);
      }
    });
  }

  const documentIds = new Set(
    Array.isArray(source.supportingDocuments)
      ? source.supportingDocuments.map((item) => item?.id)
      : [],
  );

  checkItems(errors, source, "awards", (item, base) => {
    checkText(errors, `${base}.name`, item.name, 250);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.organization`, item.organization, 250);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.description`, item.description, 1500);
    checkOption(errors, `${base}.recognitionType`,
      item.recognitionType, OPTIONS.recognitionType);
    checkOption(errors, `${base}.placement`, item.placement, OPTIONS.placement);
    checkUrl(errors, `${base}.url`, item.url);

    if (item.supportingDocumentIds !== undefined) {
      if (!Array.isArray(item.supportingDocumentIds) ||
          item.supportingDocumentIds.length > 10) {
        error(errors, `${base}.supportingDocumentIds`,
          "PROJECT_AWARD_DOCUMENT_LIMIT", "Maximum 10 document references.");
      } else {
        for (const id of item.supportingDocumentIds) {
          if (!documentIds.has(id)) {
            error(errors, `${base}.supportingDocumentIds`,
              "PROJECT_AWARD_DOCUMENT_NOT_FOUND",
              "Referenced document does not exist in this Project.");
          }
        }
      }
    }
  });

  checkItems(errors, source, "media", (item, base) => {
    checkOption(errors, `${base}.type`, item.type, OPTIONS.mediaType);
    checkOption(errors, `${base}.visibility`,
      item.visibility, OPTIONS.visibility);
    checkOption(errors, `${base}.status`,
      item.status, OPTIONS.recordStatus);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.name`, item.name, 255);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.caption`, item.caption, 1000);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.altText`, item.altText, 500);
    checkUrl(errors, `${base}.externalUrl`, item.externalUrl);

    if (!item.storageKey && !item.externalUrl) {
      error(errors, base, "PROJECT_MEDIA_SOURCE_REQUIRED",
        "A storage key or external URL is required.");
    }
  });

  checkItems(errors, source, "supportingDocuments", (item, base) => {
    checkOption(errors, `${base}.documentType`,
      item.documentType, OPTIONS.documentType);
    checkOption(errors, `${base}.visibility`,
      item.visibility, OPTIONS.visibility);
    checkOption(errors, `${base}.status`,
      item.status, OPTIONS.recordStatus);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.name`, item.name, 255);
    checkText(errors, `${base}.description`, item.description, 1000);
    checkUrl(errors, `${base}.externalUrl`, item.externalUrl);

    if (!item.storageKey && !item.externalUrl) {
      error(errors, base, "PROJECT_DOCUMENT_SOURCE_REQUIRED",
        "A storage key or external URL is required.");
    }
  });

  if (source.visibility !== undefined) {
    if (!object(source.visibility)) {
      error(errors, "visibility", "PROJECT_VISIBILITY_INVALID",
        "Visibility must be an object.");
    } else {
      for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(source.visibility)) {
        if (typeof value !== "boolean") {
          error(errors, `visibility.${name}`, "PROJECT_VISIBILITY_INVALID",
            "Visibility must be true or false.");
        }
      }
    }
  }

  if (source.presentation?.featuredMediaId) {
    const featuredId = source.presentation.featuredMediaId;
    const eligible = Array.isArray(source.media) &&
      source.media.some((item) =>
        item?.id === featuredId && item.status !== "archived" &&
        item.visibility !== "private" &&
        (item.storageKey || item.externalUrl),
      );

    if (!eligible) {
      error(errors, "presentation.featuredMediaId",
        "PROJECT_FEATURED_MEDIA_INVALID",
        "Featured media must be active, public, and available.");
    }
  }

  return errors;
}

FILE: D:\Documents\Project\Portfolio\portfolio-plateform\backend\src\schemas\projectSchemas.js
============================================================
import { validateNestedProject } from "./projectNestedValidation.js";

const PROJECT_ID_PATTERN =
  /^project-(?:[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}|[0-9]{10,17}-[a-z0-9]{1,20})$/i;

const CATEGORIES = new Set([
  "software",
  "web-application",
  "mobile-application",
  "artificial-intelligence",
  "data",
  "cloud-infrastructure",
  "networking",
  "cybersecurity",
  "embedded-systems",
  "electronics",
  "automation",
  "internet-of-things",
  "research",
  "design",
  "business",
  "academic",
  "community",
  "other",
]);

const LIFECYCLE_STATUSES = new Set([
  "planned",
  "in-progress",
  "completed",
  "maintained",
  "paused",
  "cancelled",
]);

const RECORD_STATUSES = new Set(["active", "archived"]);

const SERVER_OWNED_FIELDS = [
  "userId",
  "user_id",
  "createdAt",
  "updatedAt",
  "rowVersion",
];

function createValidationError(errors) {
  const error = new Error("Project validation failed.");

  error.name = "ProjectValidationError";
  error.code = "PROJECT_VALIDATION_FAILED";
  error.status = 400;
  error.publicMessage = "Please correct the Project information.";
  error.errors = errors;

  error.fieldErrors = {};

  for (const item of errors) {
    if (!error.fieldErrors[item.field]) {
      error.fieldErrors[item.field] = item.message;
    }
  }

  return error;
}

function addError(errors, field, code, message) {
  errors.push({ field, code, message });
}

function isPlainObject(value) {
  return value !== null && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value);
}

function isValidProjectDate(value) {
  if (typeof value !== "string") {
    return false;
  }

  const match = value.match(
    /^(\d{4})-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])(?:-(0[1-9]|[12]\d|3[01]))?$/,
  );

  if (!match) {
    return false;
  }

  const year = Number(match[1]);
  const month = Number(match[2]);
  const day = match[3] ? Number(match[3]) : 1;

  const date = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));

  return (
    date.getUTCFullYear() === year &&
    date.getUTCMonth() === month - 1 &&
    date.getUTCDate() === day
  );
}

export function parseProjectInput(value) {
  const errors = [];

  if (!isPlainObject(value)) {
    throw createValidationError([
      {
        field: "project",
        code: "PROJECT_OBJECT_REQUIRED",
        message: "Project data must be an object.",
      },
    ]);
  }

  for (const field of SERVER_OWNED_FIELDS) {
    if (Object.hasOwn(value, field)) {
      addError(
        errors,
        field,
        "PROJECT_SERVER_FIELD_FORBIDDEN",
        `${field} cannot be supplied by the client.`,
      );
    }
  }

  if (typeof value.id !== "string" || !PROJECT_ID_PATTERN.test(value.id)) {
    addError(
      errors,
      "id",
      "PROJECT_ID_INVALID",
      "A valid Project identifier is required.",
    );
  }

  if (
    typeof value.title !== "string" ||
    !value.title.trim() ||
    value.title.trim().length > 200
  ) {
    addError(
      errors,
      "title",
      "PROJECT_TITLE_INVALID",
      "Project title must contain 1 to 200 characters.",
    );
  }

  if (
    typeof value.role !== "string" ||
    !value.role.trim() ||
    value.role.trim().length > 160
  ) {
    addError(
      errors,
      "role",
      "PROJECT_ROLE_INVALID",
      "Project role must contain 1 to 160 characters.",
    );
  }

  if (!CATEGORIES.has(value.category)) {
    addError(
      errors,
      "category",
      "PROJECT_CATEGORY_INVALID",
      "Select a valid Project category.",
    );
  }

  if (!LIFECYCLE_STATUSES.has(value.lifecycleStatus)) {
    addError(
      errors,
      "lifecycleStatus",
      "PROJECT_LIFECYCLE_INVALID",
      "Select a valid lifecycle status.",
    );
  }

  if (!RECORD_STATUSES.has(value.recordStatus)) {
    addError(
      errors,
      "recordStatus",
      "PROJECT_RECORD_STATUS_INVALID",
      "Select a valid record status.",
    );
  }

  if (!isPlainObject(value.dates)) {
    addError(
      errors,
      "dates",
      "PROJECT_DATES_INVALID",
      "Project dates must be an object.",
    );
  } else {
    if (!isValidProjectDate(value.dates.startDate)) {
      addError(
        errors,
        "dates.startDate",
        "PROJECT_START_DATE_INVALID",
        "A valid Project start date is required.",
      );
    }

    if (value.dates.endDate && !isValidProjectDate(value.dates.endDate)) {
      addError(
        errors,
        "dates.endDate",
        "PROJECT_END_DATE_INVALID",
        "Enter a valid Project end date.",
      );
    }

    if (value.dates.isCurrent === true && value.dates.endDate) {
      addError(
        errors,
        "dates.endDate",
        "PROJECT_CURRENT_END_DATE_CONFLICT",
        "An ongoing Project cannot have an end date.",
      );
    }

    if (
      value.dates.isCurrent === true &&
      !["in-progress", "maintained"].includes(value.lifecycleStatus)
    ) {
      addError(
        errors,
        "lifecycleStatus",
        "PROJECT_CURRENT_LIFECYCLE_CONFLICT",
        "An ongoing Project must be In Progress or Maintained.",
      );
    }

    if (
      ["completed", "cancelled"].includes(value.lifecycleStatus) &&
      value.dates.isCurrent !== true &&
      !value.dates.endDate
    ) {
      addError(
        errors,
        "dates.endDate",
        "PROJECT_END_DATE_REQUIRED",
        "A completion or cancellation date is required.",
      );
    }

    if (
      isValidProjectDate(value.dates.startDate) &&
      isValidProjectDate(value.dates.endDate)
    ) {
      const start = new Date(
        value.dates.startDate.length === 7
          ? `${value.dates.startDate}-01`
          : value.dates.startDate,
      );

      const end = new Date(
        value.dates.endDate.length === 7
          ? `${value.dates.endDate}-01`
          : value.dates.endDate,
      );

      if (end < start) {
        addError(
          errors,
          "dates.endDate",
          "PROJECT_DATE_ORDER_INVALID",
          "End date cannot be earlier than start date.",
        );
      }
    }
  }

  if (
    !isPlainObject(value.presentation) ||
    typeof value.presentation.isFeatured !== "boolean"
  ) {
    addError(
      errors,
      "presentation.isFeatured",
      "PROJECT_FEATURED_INVALID",
      "Project featured status must be a boolean.",
    );
  }

  const urlFields = [
    ["organization.website", value.organization?.website],
    ...[
      "liveUrl",
      "repositoryUrl",
      "documentationUrl",
      "caseStudyUrl",
      "videoUrl",
    ].map((name) => [`links.${name}`, value.links?.[name]]),
  ];

  for (const [field, urlValue] of urlFields) {
    if (urlValue === undefined || urlValue === "") continue;

    let valid = false;

    if (typeof urlValue === "string") {
      try {
        const parsed = new URL(urlValue);
        valid = ["http:", "https:"].includes(parsed.protocol);
      } catch {
        valid = false;
      }
    }

    if (!valid) {
      addError(
        errors,
        field,
        "PROJECT_URL_INVALID",
        "Enter a valid HTTP or HTTPS URL.",
      );
    }
  }

  const collectionLimits = [
    ["problem.objectives", value.problem?.objectives, 25],
    ["problem.constraints", value.problem?.constraints, 25],
    ["solution.features", value.solution?.features, 40],
    ["solution.contributions", value.solution?.contributions, 40],
    ["results.metrics", value.results?.metrics, 25],
    ["skillRelationships", value.skillRelationships, 60],
    ["technologies", value.technologies, 60],
    ["awards", value.awards, 20],
    ["media", value.media, 30],
    ["supportingDocuments", value.supportingDocuments, 30],
    ...[
      "experienceRelationships",
      "educationRelationships",
      "trainingRelationships",
      "certificationRelationships",
    ].map((name) => [
      `relatedRecords.${name}`,
      value.relatedRecords?.[name],
      50,
    ]),
  ];

  for (const [field, items, maximum] of collectionLimits) {
    if (items === undefined) continue;

    if (!Array.isArray(items) || items.length > maximum) {
      addError(
        errors,
        field,
        "PROJECT_COLLECTION_INVALID",
        `This collection must contain at most ${maximum} items.`,
      );
    }
  }
  errors.push(...validateNestedProject(value));

  if (errors.length > 0) {
    throw createValidationError(errors);
  }

  return structuredClone(value);
}

export function parseProjectRowVersion(value) {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || !/^[A-Za-z0-9+/]{11}=$/.test(value)) {
    throw createValidationError([
      {
        field: "rowVersion",
        code: "PROJECT_VERSION_INVALID",
        message: "A valid Project version is required.",
      },
    ]);
  }

  return value;
}

import { z } from "zod";

import {
  MAXIMUM_PASSWORD_LENGTH,
  MINIMUM_PASSWORD_LENGTH,
} from "../security/passwordSecurity.js";

/*
 * =========================================
 * Shared Registration Fields
 * =========================================
 */

const emailSchema = z
  .string({
    error: "Email is required.",
  })
  .trim()
  .min(1, "Email is required.")
  .max(320, "Email cannot contain more than 320 characters.")
  .email("Enter a valid email address.")
  .transform((email) => email.toLowerCase());

const challengeIdSchema = z
  .string({
    error: "Registration challenge ID is required.",
  })
  .trim()
  .uuid("Registration challenge ID must be a valid UUID.");

const otpSchema = z
  .string({
    error: "Verification code is required.",
  })
  .trim()
  .regex(/^\d{6}$/, "Verification code must contain exactly six digits.");

const continuationTokenSchema = z
  .string({
    error: "Registration continuation token is required.",
  })
  .trim()
  .min(32, "Registration continuation token is invalid.")
  .max(500, "Registration continuation token is invalid.");

const passwordSchema = z
  .string({
    error: "Password is required.",
  })
  .min(
    MINIMUM_PASSWORD_LENGTH,
    `Password must contain at least ${MINIMUM_PASSWORD_LENGTH} characters.`,
  )
  .max(
    MAXIMUM_PASSWORD_LENGTH,
    `Password cannot contain more than ${MAXIMUM_PASSWORD_LENGTH} characters.`,
  );

/*
 * =========================================
 * Request Registration OTP
 * =========================================
 *
 * POST /api/auth/register/request
 */

export const registrationRequestSchema = z
  .object({
    email: emailSchema,
  })
  .strict();

/*
 * =========================================
 * Verify Registration OTP
 * =========================================
 *
 * POST /api/auth/register/verify
 */

export const registrationVerificationSchema = z
  .object({
    challengeId: challengeIdSchema,

    otp: otpSchema,
  })
  .strict();

/*
 * =========================================
 * Complete Registration
 * =========================================
 *
 * POST /api/auth/register/complete
 */

export const registrationCompletionSchema = z
  .object({
    challengeId: challengeIdSchema,

    continuationToken: continuationTokenSchema,

    password: passwordSchema,
  })
  .strict();

/*
 * =========================================
 * Zod Field Error Mapping
 * =========================================
 */

function mapFieldErrors(issues) {
  const fieldErrors = {};

  for (const issue of issues) {
    const fieldName = issue.path.length > 0 ? issue.path.join(".") : "_form";

    if (!fieldErrors[fieldName]) {
      fieldErrors[fieldName] = [];
    }

    fieldErrors[fieldName].push(issue.message);
  }

  return fieldErrors;
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Validation Error Creation
 * =========================================
 */

function createValidationError(zodError) {
  const errors = zodError.issues.map((issue) => ({
    field: issue.path.length > 0 ? issue.path.join(".") : "",

    code: issue.code,

    message: issue.message,
  }));

  const error = new Error("Registration request validation failed.");

  error.name = "RegistrationValidationError";

  error.code = "REGISTRATION_VALIDATION_FAILED";

  error.status = 400;

  error.publicMessage = "Review the registration information and try again.";

  error.errors = errors;

  error.fieldErrors = mapFieldErrors(zodError.issues);

  return error;
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Parse Registration Input
 * =========================================
 *
 * This helper keeps Zod-specific logic out
 * of the Express controllers.
 */

export function parseRegistrationInput(schema, input) {
  const result = schema.safeParse(input);

  if (!result.success) {
    throw createValidationError(result.error);
  }

  return result.data;
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Individual Parsing Helpers
 * =========================================
 */

export function parseRegistrationRequest(input) {
  return parseRegistrationInput(registrationRequestSchema, input);
}

export function parseRegistrationVerification(input) {
  return parseRegistrationInput(registrationVerificationSchema, input);
}

export function parseRegistrationCompletion(input) {
  return parseRegistrationInput(registrationCompletionSchema, input);
}

import { z } from "zod";

/*
 * =========================================
 * Shared Request Fields
 * =========================================
 */

const shortText = z.string().trim().max(500).default("");

const longText = z.string().trim().max(5000).default("");

const stringList = z.array(z.string().trim().max(500)).max(100).default([]);

const trainingSchema = z.object({
  name: shortText,
  organization: shortText,
  description: longText,
});

/*
 * =========================================
 * Resume Context
 * =========================================
 */

const experienceSchema = z.object({
  position: shortText,
  company: shortText,
  description: longText,

  achievements: z.array(z.string().trim().max(1500)).max(30).default([]),
});

const projectSchema = z.object({
  name: shortText,
  description: longText,
  technologies: stringList,
});

const educationSchema = z.object({
  degree: shortText,
  fieldOfStudy: shortText,
  institution: shortText,
});

const certificationSchema = z.object({
  name: shortText,
  organization: shortText,
});

/*
 * =========================================
 * API Request Schema
 * =========================================
 */

export const resumeSummaryRequestSchema = z.object({
  documentType: z.enum(["resume", "portfolio"]).default("resume"),

  generationPurpose: z.enum(["summary", "about", "tagline"]).default("summary"),

  targetRole: shortText,

  template: z
    .enum(["professional", "modern", "technical", "academic"])
    .default("professional"),

  professionalTitle: shortText,

  skills: stringList,

  experiences: z.array(experienceSchema).max(50).default([]),

  projects: z.array(projectSchema).max(50).default([]),

  education: z.array(educationSchema).max(30).default([]),

  certifications: z.array(certificationSchema).max(50).default([]),

  trainings: z.array(trainingSchema).max(50).default([]),

  interests: z.array(z.string().trim().max(500)).max(100).default([]),

  professionalGoals: z.string().trim().max(3000).default(""),

  existingSummary: z.string().trim().max(3000).default(""),

  generationRules: z
    .object({
      useOnlyProvidedFacts: z.boolean().default(true),

      doNotInventQualifications: z.boolean().default(true),

      doNotInventMetrics: z.boolean().default(true),

      numberOfSuggestions: z.number().int().min(1).max(3).default(3),

      recommendedWords: z.string().trim().max(50).default("55â€“80 words"),
    })
    .optional(),
});

/*
 * =========================================
 * Structured AI Response Schema
 * =========================================
 *
 * Avoid .min(), .max(), and .length() here.
 * OpenAI Structured Outputs does not support
 * every JSON Schema array constraint.
 */

export const resumeSummaryResponseSchema = z.object({
  suggestions: z.array(
    z.object({
      id: z.string(),
      label: z.string(),
      text: z.string(),
    }),
  ),

  warnings: z.array(z.string()),
});

/*
 * =========================================
 * Validate and Normalize AI Response
 * =========================================
 */

export function normalizeResumeSummaryResponse(value) {
  /*
   * Some providers may return the suggestions array
   * directly instead of wrapping it in an object.
   */

  const rawSuggestions = Array.isArray(value) ? value : value?.suggestions;

  if (!Array.isArray(rawSuggestions)) {
    const error = new Error(
      "The AI response does not contain a suggestions array.",
    );

    error.status = 502;
    error.publicMessage =
      "The AI returned an invalid response. Please try again.";

    throw error;
  }

  /*
   * Normalize small provider differences.
   *
   * Supported suggestion formats:
   *
   * { id, label, text }
   * { id, label, summary }
   * "Summary text"
   */

  const suggestions = rawSuggestions
    .map((suggestion, index) => {
      if (typeof suggestion === "string") {
        return {
          id: `summary-suggestion-${index + 1}`,
          label: `Option ${index + 1}`,
          text: suggestion.trim(),
        };
      }

      if (!suggestion || typeof suggestion !== "object") {
        return null;
      }

      const text = String(
        suggestion.text || suggestion.summary || suggestion.content || "",
      ).trim();

      if (!text) {
        return null;
      }

      return {
        id: String(suggestion.id || `summary-suggestion-${index + 1}`).trim(),

        label: String(
          suggestion.label || suggestion.title || `Option ${index + 1}`,
        ).trim(),

        text,
      };
    })
    .filter(Boolean)
    .slice(0, 3);

  if (suggestions.length !== 3) {
    const error = new Error(
      `Expected 3 valid suggestions but received ${suggestions.length}.`,
    );

    error.status = 502;
    error.publicMessage =
      "The AI did not return three valid suggestions. Please regenerate them.";

    throw error;
  }

  /*
   * Warnings are useful but should not cause the
   * entire request to fail if Groq omits them.
   */

  const rawWarnings = Array.isArray(value?.warnings) ? value.warnings : [];

  const warnings = rawWarnings
    .map((warning) => {
      if (typeof warning === "string") {
        return warning.trim();
      }

      if (warning && typeof warning === "object") {
        return String(warning.message || warning.text || "").trim();
      }

      return "";
    })
    .filter(Boolean)
    .slice(0, 5);

  return {
    suggestions,
    warnings,
  };
}

import { z } from "zod";

/*
 * =========================================
 * SQL Server Unique Identifier
 * =========================================
 *
 * SQL Server UNIQUEIDENTIFIER values use the
 * familiar 8-4-4-4-12 hexadecimal structure.
 *
 * Azure SQL may generate identifiers that do
 * not satisfy Zod's stricter RFC UUID version
 * validation. Therefore, validate the SQL
 * identifier structure without restricting
 * its version or variant bits.
 */

const SQL_UNIQUE_IDENTIFIER_PATTERN =
  /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;

/*
 * =========================================
 * Session Management Fields
 * =========================================
 */

const sessionIdSchema = z
  .string({
    error: "Session ID is required.",
  })
  .trim()
  .min(1, "Session ID is required.")
  .regex(
    SQL_UNIQUE_IDENTIFIER_PATTERN,

    "Session ID must be a valid unique identifier.",
  )
  .transform((sessionId) => sessionId.toLowerCase());

/*
 * =========================================
 * Revoke Session
 * =========================================
 */

const revokeSessionSchema = z
  .object({
    sessionId: sessionIdSchema,
  })
  .strict();

/*
 * =========================================
 * Field Error Mapping
 * =========================================
 */

function mapFieldErrors(issues) {
  const fieldErrors = {};

  for (const issue of issues) {
    const fieldName = issue.path.length > 0 ? issue.path.join(".") : "_form";

    if (!fieldErrors[fieldName]) {
      fieldErrors[fieldName] = [];
    }

    fieldErrors[fieldName].push(issue.message);
  }

  return fieldErrors;
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Validation Error
 * =========================================
 */

function createValidationError(zodError) {
  const error = new Error("Session-management validation failed.");

  error.name = "SessionManagementValidationError";

  error.code = "SESSION_MANAGEMENT_VALIDATION_FAILED";

  error.status = 400;

  error.publicMessage = "Review the session information and try again.";

  error.errors = zodError.issues.map((issue) => ({
    field: issue.path.length > 0 ? issue.path.join(".") : "",

    code: issue.code,

    message: issue.message,
  }));

  error.fieldErrors = mapFieldErrors(zodError.issues);

  return error;
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Parse Revoke Session Parameters
 * =========================================
 */

export function parseRevokeSessionParameters(input) {
  const result = revokeSessionSchema.safeParse(input);

  if (!result.success) {
    throw createValidationError(result.error);
  }

  return result.data;
}
