import {
  listProjectsByUser,
  findProjectById,
  createProject,
  updateProject,
  deleteProject,
} from "../../repositories/projectRepository.js";

import {
  parseProjectInput,
  parseProjectRowVersion,
} from "../../schemas/projectSchemas.js";

const SERVER_OWNED_FIELDS = [
  "userId",
  "user_id",
  "createdAt",
  "updatedAt",
  "rowVersion",
];

function createProjectError({ message, code, status, publicMessage }) {
  const error = new Error(message);
  error.name = "ProjectServiceError";
  error.code = code;
  error.status = status;
  error.publicMessage = publicMessage;
  return error;
}

function assertUserId(userId) {
  if (typeof userId !== "string" || !userId.trim()) {
    throw createProjectError({
      message: "An authenticated user ID is required.",
      code: "PROJECT_AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED",
      status: 401,
      publicMessage: "You must sign in to access your Projects.",
    });
  }

  return userId.trim();
}

function assertProjectId(projectId) {
  if (
    typeof projectId !== "string" ||
    !projectId.trim() ||
    projectId.length > 200
  ) {
    throw createProjectError({
      message: "A valid Project ID is required.",
      code: "PROJECT_ID_INVALID",
      status: 400,
      publicMessage: "The Project identifier is invalid.",
    });
  }

  return projectId.trim();
}

function projectNotFound() {
  return createProjectError({
    message: "Project not found for authenticated user.",
    code: "PROJECT_NOT_FOUND",
    status: 404,
    publicMessage: "The requested Project could not be found.",
  });
}

function projectVersionConflict() {
  return createProjectError({
    message: "Project version conflict.",
    code: "PROJECT_VERSION_CONFLICT",
    status: 409,
    publicMessage:
      "This Project was changed elsewhere. Reload it before saving.",
  });
}

function projectIdMismatch() {
  return createProjectError({
    message: "The Project body ID does not match the requested Project ID.",
    code: "PROJECT_ID_MISMATCH",
    status: 400,
    publicMessage: "The Project identifiers do not match.",
  });
}

function withoutServerFields(project) {
  const result = { ...project };

  for (const field of SERVER_OWNED_FIELDS) {
    delete result[field];
  }

  return result;
}

export async function getAuthenticatedProjects({ userId }) {
  return listProjectsByUser(assertUserId(userId));
}

export async function getAuthenticatedProject({ userId, projectId }) {
  const project = await findProjectById(
    assertUserId(userId),
    assertProjectId(projectId),
  );

  if (!project) {
    throw projectNotFound();
  }

  return project;
}

export async function createAuthenticatedProject({ userId, project }) {
  const authenticatedUserId = assertUserId(userId);

  // Reject invalid data before any database operation.
  // Client-supplied server-owned fields remain forbidden.
  const validatedProject = parseProjectInput(project);

  try {
    return await createProject(authenticatedUserId, validatedProject);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.number === 2627 || error.number === 2601) {
      throw createProjectError({
        message: "Project ID already exists.",
        code: "PROJECT_ALREADY_EXISTS",
        status: 409,
        publicMessage: "A Project with this identifier already exists.",
      });
    }

    throw error;
  }
}

export async function updateAuthenticatedProject({
  userId,
  projectId,
  project,
  rowVersion,
}) {
  const authenticatedUserId = assertUserId(userId);
  const normalizedProjectId = assertProjectId(projectId);

  // Validate the supplied version and full Project body.
  const expectedRowVersion = parseProjectRowVersion(rowVersion);
  const validatedProject = parseProjectInput(project);

  if (validatedProject.id !== normalizedProjectId) {
    throw projectIdMismatch();
  }

  const existingProject = await findProjectById(
    authenticatedUserId,
    normalizedProjectId,
  );

  if (!existingProject) {
    throw projectNotFound();
  }

  const updatedProject = await updateProject(
    authenticatedUserId,
    validatedProject,
    expectedRowVersion,
  );

  if (!updatedProject) {
    throw projectVersionConflict();
  }

  return updatedProject;
}

export async function setAuthenticatedProjectArchiveStatus({
  userId,
  projectId,
  rowVersion,
  archived,
}) {
  const authenticatedUserId = assertUserId(userId);
  const normalizedProjectId = assertProjectId(projectId);
  const expectedRowVersion = parseProjectRowVersion(rowVersion);

  if (typeof archived !== "boolean") {
    throw createProjectError({
      message: "Archive status must be a boolean.",
      code: "PROJECT_ARCHIVE_STATUS_INVALID",
      status: 400,
      publicMessage: "A valid archive status is required.",
    });
  }

  const existingProject = await findProjectById(
    authenticatedUserId,
    normalizedProjectId,
  );

  if (!existingProject) {
    throw projectNotFound();
  }

  // Database-managed metadata is not editable Project content.
  const editableProject = withoutServerFields(existingProject);

  const validatedProject = parseProjectInput({
    ...editableProject,
    recordStatus: archived ? "archived" : "active",
  });

  // SQL Server must match the caller's row version.
  // Do not substitute the version from the latest database read.
  const updatedProject = await updateProject(
    authenticatedUserId,
    validatedProject,
    expectedRowVersion,
  );

  if (!updatedProject) {
    throw projectVersionConflict();
  }

  return updatedProject;
}

export async function deleteAuthenticatedProject({
  userId,
  projectId,
  rowVersion,
}) {
  const authenticatedUserId = assertUserId(userId);
  const normalizedProjectId = assertProjectId(projectId);
  const expectedRowVersion = parseProjectRowVersion(rowVersion);

  const existingProject = await findProjectById(
    authenticatedUserId,
    normalizedProjectId,
  );

  if (!existingProject) {
    throw projectNotFound();
  }

  const deleted = await deleteProject(
    authenticatedUserId,
    normalizedProjectId,
    expectedRowVersion,
  );

  if (!deleted) {
    throw projectVersionConflict();
  }

  return { deleted: true };
}

import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";

import {
  createLoginAuditEvent,
  createRegistrationChallenge,
  deleteOpenRegistrationChallenges,
  deleteRegistrationChallengeById,
  getRegistrationChallengeById,
  incrementRegistrationChallengeAttempt,
  markRegistrationChallengeVerified,
  completeRegistration as completeRegistrationRepository,
} from "../../repositories/authRepository.js";

import { assertValidEmail, maskEmail } from "../../security/emailSecurity.js";

import {
  generateOtp,
  hashOtp,
  isValidOtpFormat,
  verifyOtp,
} from "../../security/otpSecurity.js";

import { hashPassword } from "../../security/passwordSecurity.js";

import {
  createOpaqueTokenRecord,
  hashToken,
} from "../../security/tokenSecurity.js";

import {
  getRequestIpHash,
  getRequestUserAgent,
} from "../../security/requestSecurity.js";

/*
 * =========================================
 * Registration Configuration
 * =========================================
 */

const REGISTRATION_PURPOSE = "registration";

const REGISTRATION_CONTINUATION_PURPOSE = "registration-continuation";

const REGISTRATION_OTP_EXPIRATION_MINUTES = 10;

const REGISTRATION_CONTINUATION_EXPIRATION_MINUTES = 15;

const REGISTRATION_MAXIMUM_ATTEMPTS = 5;

/*
 * =========================================
 * Public Responses
 * =========================================
 */

const REGISTRATION_REQUEST_MESSAGE =
  "If the email address can be registered, a verification code will be sent.";

const INVALID_CHALLENGE_MESSAGE =
  "The verification request is invalid or expired. Start registration again.";

const INVALID_OTP_MESSAGE = "The verification code is invalid or expired.";

const INVALID_CONTINUATION_MESSAGE =
  "The registration session is invalid or expired. Start registration again.";

/*
 * =========================================
 * Date Helpers
 * =========================================
 */

function addMinutes(date, minutes) {
  return new Date(date.getTime() + minutes * 60 * 1000);
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Service Error
 * =========================================
 */

function createRegistrationError({
  message,
  code,
  status = 400,
  publicMessage,
  details,
}) {
  const error = new Error(message);

  error.name = "RegistrationError";
  error.code = code;
  error.status = status;
  error.publicMessage = publicMessage;
  error.details = details || null;

  return error;
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Challenge ID Validation
 * =========================================
 */

function assertChallengeId(challengeId) {
  const value = typeof challengeId === "string" ? challengeId.trim() : "";

  const uuidPattern =
    /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-5][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;

  if (!uuidPattern.test(value)) {
    throw createRegistrationError({
      message: "The registration challenge ID is invalid.",

      code: "REGISTRATION_CHALLENGE_INVALID",

      publicMessage: INVALID_CHALLENGE_MESSAGE,
    });
  }

  return value.toLowerCase();
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Continuation Token Validation
 * =========================================
 */

function assertContinuationToken(token) {
  const value = typeof token === "string" ? token.trim() : "";

  if (value.length < 32 || value.length > 500) {
    throw createRegistrationError({
      message: "The registration continuation token is invalid.",

      code: "REGISTRATION_CONTINUATION_INVALID",

      publicMessage: INVALID_CONTINUATION_MESSAGE,
    });
  }

  return value;
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Safe Audit Recording
 * =========================================
 *
 * An audit-write failure should be recorded
 * by the server, but it should not hide the
 * original registration result.
 */

async function recordRegistrationAudit(event) {
  try {
    return await createLoginAuditEvent(event);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Unable to record registration audit event:", {
      eventType: event.eventType,
      message: error.message,
    });

    return null;
  }
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Request Registration OTP
 * =========================================
 *
 * sendRegistrationOtp must be an async
 * function supplied by the email layer:
 *
 * async function sendRegistrationOtp({
 *   email,
 *   otp,
 *   expiresInMinutes,
 * }) {}
 */

export async function requestRegistration({
  email,
  request,
  sendRegistrationOtp,
}) {
  const normalizedEmail = assertValidEmail(email);

  if (typeof sendRegistrationOtp !== "function") {
    throw createRegistrationError({
      message: "A registration OTP email sender was not provided.",

      code: "REGISTRATION_EMAIL_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE",

      status: 503,

      publicMessage:
        "Email verification is temporarily unavailable. Please try again later.",
    });
  }

  const challengeId = randomUUID();

  const otp = generateOtp();

  const codeHash = hashOtp({
    challengeId,
    purpose: REGISTRATION_PURPOSE,
    otp,
  });

  const now = new Date();

  const expiresAt = addMinutes(now, REGISTRATION_OTP_EXPIRATION_MINUTES);

  const requestIpHash = getRequestIpHash(request);

  const userAgent = getRequestUserAgent(request);

  /*
   * A new registration request invalidates
   * older incomplete challenges for the same
   * email address.
   */

  await deleteOpenRegistrationChallenges(normalizedEmail);

  const challenge = await createRegistrationChallenge({
    id: challengeId,

    email: normalizedEmail,
    normalizedEmail,

    codeHash,

    expiresAt,
    lastSentAt: now,

    maximumAttempts: REGISTRATION_MAXIMUM_ATTEMPTS,

    requestIpHash,
  });

  try {
    /*
     * The plaintext OTP is passed directly to
     * the email provider. It is never returned
     * to the frontend or stored in SQL Server.
     */

    await sendRegistrationOtp({
      email: normalizedEmail,

      otp,

      expiresInMinutes: REGISTRATION_OTP_EXPIRATION_MINUTES,
    });
  } catch (emailError) {
    /*
     * A challenge should not remain usable if
     * its email could not be delivered.
     */

    await deleteRegistrationChallengeById(challenge.id).catch(
      (cleanupError) => {
        console.error(
          "Unable to remove an undelivered registration challenge:",
          cleanupError,
        );
      },
    );

    await recordRegistrationAudit({
      emailAttempted: normalizedEmail,

      eventType: "registration_requested",

      wasSuccessful: false,

      failureReason: "email_delivery_failed",

      ipAddressHash: requestIpHash,

      userAgent,
    });

    throw createRegistrationError({
      message: "The registration verification email could not be sent.",

      code: "REGISTRATION_EMAIL_DELIVERY_FAILED",

      status: 503,

      publicMessage:
        "The verification email could not be sent. Please try again later.",

      details: {
        cause: emailError.message,
      },
    });
  }

  await recordRegistrationAudit({
    emailAttempted: normalizedEmail,

    eventType: "registration_requested",

    wasSuccessful: true,

    ipAddressHash: requestIpHash,

    userAgent,
  });

  return {
    challengeId: challenge.id,

    email: maskEmail(normalizedEmail),

    expiresAt: challenge.expiresAt,

    maximumAttempts: challenge.maximumAttempts,

    message: REGISTRATION_REQUEST_MESSAGE,
  };
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Verify Registration OTP
 * =========================================
 */

export async function verifyRegistrationOtp({ challengeId, otp, request }) {
  const normalizedChallengeId = assertChallengeId(challengeId);

  const requestIpHash = getRequestIpHash(request);

  const userAgent = getRequestUserAgent(request);

  if (!isValidOtpFormat(otp)) {
    throw createRegistrationError({
      message: "The submitted registration OTP has an invalid format.",

      code: "REGISTRATION_OTP_INVALID",

      publicMessage: INVALID_OTP_MESSAGE,
    });
  }

  const challenge = await getRegistrationChallengeById(normalizedChallengeId);

  if (!challenge) {
    throw createRegistrationError({
      message: "The registration challenge does not exist.",

      code: "REGISTRATION_CHALLENGE_NOT_FOUND",

      publicMessage: INVALID_CHALLENGE_MESSAGE,
    });
  }

  const auditBase = {
    emailAttempted: challenge.normalizedPendingEmail,

    eventType: "registration_otp_verified",

    ipAddressHash: requestIpHash,

    userAgent,
  };

  /*
   * Already consumed challenges cannot be
   * reused.
   */

  if (challenge.consumedAt) {
    await recordRegistrationAudit({
      ...auditBase,

      wasSuccessful: false,

      failureReason: "challenge_consumed",
    });

    throw createRegistrationError({
      message: "The registration challenge was already consumed.",

      code: "REGISTRATION_CHALLENGE_CONSUMED",

      publicMessage: INVALID_CHALLENGE_MESSAGE,
    });
  }

  /*
   * A verified challenge should not accept a
   * second OTP verification.
   */

  if (challenge.verifiedAt) {
    await recordRegistrationAudit({
      ...auditBase,

      wasSuccessful: false,

      failureReason: "challenge_already_verified",
    });

    throw createRegistrationError({
      message: "The registration challenge was already verified.",

      code: "REGISTRATION_CHALLENGE_ALREADY_VERIFIED",

      publicMessage: "This verification code has already been used.",
    });
  }

  const now = new Date();

  if (!challenge.expiresAt || new Date(challenge.expiresAt) <= now) {
    await recordRegistrationAudit({
      ...auditBase,

      wasSuccessful: false,

      failureReason: "challenge_expired",
    });

    throw createRegistrationError({
      message: "The registration challenge has expired.",

      code: "REGISTRATION_CHALLENGE_EXPIRED",

      publicMessage: INVALID_OTP_MESSAGE,
    });
  }

  if (challenge.attemptCount >= challenge.maximumAttempts) {
    await recordRegistrationAudit({
      ...auditBase,

      wasSuccessful: false,

      failureReason: "maximum_attempts_reached",
    });

    throw createRegistrationError({
      message: "The registration challenge has reached its maximum attempts.",

      code: "REGISTRATION_MAXIMUM_ATTEMPTS_REACHED",

      status: 429,

      publicMessage:
        "Too many incorrect verification attempts. Start registration again.",
    });
  }

  const otpMatches = verifyOtp({
    challengeId: normalizedChallengeId,

    purpose: REGISTRATION_PURPOSE,

    otp,

    expectedHash: challenge.codeHash,
  });

  if (!otpMatches) {
    const attempt = await incrementRegistrationChallengeAttempt(
      normalizedChallengeId,
    );

    const attemptsRemaining = attempt
      ? Math.max(0, attempt.maximumAttempts - attempt.attemptCount)
      : 0;

    await recordRegistrationAudit({
      ...auditBase,

      wasSuccessful: false,

      failureReason: "incorrect_otp",
    });

    if (attemptsRemaining === 0) {
      throw createRegistrationError({
        message: "The registration challenge has reached its maximum attempts.",

        code: "REGISTRATION_MAXIMUM_ATTEMPTS_REACHED",

        status: 429,

        publicMessage:
          "Too many incorrect verification attempts. Start registration again.",
      });
    }

    throw createRegistrationError({
      message: "The submitted registration OTP is incorrect.",

      code: "REGISTRATION_OTP_INCORRECT",

      publicMessage: INVALID_OTP_MESSAGE,

      details: {
        attemptsRemaining,
      },
    });
  }

  /*
   * The OTP is correct. Create an opaque token
   * that authorizes only the password-setup
   * stage.
   */

  const continuation = createOpaqueTokenRecord(
    REGISTRATION_CONTINUATION_PURPOSE,
  );

  const continuationExpiresAt = addMinutes(
    now,
    REGISTRATION_CONTINUATION_EXPIRATION_MINUTES,
  );

  const verifiedChallenge = await markRegistrationChallengeVerified({
    challengeId: normalizedChallengeId,

    continuationTokenHash: continuation.hash,

    continuationExpiresAt,
  });

  /*
   * The update can return null when another
   * request verified or invalidated the same
   * challenge concurrently.
   */

  if (!verifiedChallenge) {
    await recordRegistrationAudit({
      ...auditBase,

      wasSuccessful: false,

      failureReason: "challenge_state_changed",
    });

    throw createRegistrationError({
      message:
        "The registration challenge could not be verified because its state changed.",

      code: "REGISTRATION_CHALLENGE_STATE_CHANGED",

      status: 409,

      publicMessage:
        "This verification request is no longer available. Start registration again.",
    });
  }

  await recordRegistrationAudit({
    ...auditBase,

    wasSuccessful: true,
  });

  return {
    challengeId: verifiedChallenge.id,

    continuationToken: continuation.token,

    email: maskEmail(verifiedChallenge.normalizedPendingEmail),

    expiresAt: verifiedChallenge.expiresAt,

    message: "Email verified. Create your password to finish registration.",
  };
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Complete Registration
 * =========================================
 */

export async function completeRegistration({
  challengeId,
  continuationToken,
  password,
}) {
  const normalizedChallengeId = assertChallengeId(challengeId);

  const normalizedContinuationToken =
    assertContinuationToken(continuationToken);

  /*
   * Password validation occurs inside
   * hashPassword. Invalid passwords produce a
   * safe PasswordValidationError.
   */

  const passwordHash = await hashPassword(password);

  /*
   * Only the continuation token hash is sent
   * to the repository and compared with the
   * stored hash.
   */

  const continuationTokenHash = hashToken(
    normalizedContinuationToken,
    REGISTRATION_CONTINUATION_PURPOSE,
  );

  const result = await completeRegistrationRepository({
    challengeId: normalizedChallengeId,

    continuationTokenHash,

    passwordHash,
  });

  return {
    user: result.user,

    roles: result.roles,

    message: "Your account was created successfully.",
  };
}

import { generateResumeSummaryWithGroq } from "../providers/groqResumeSummaryProvider.js";

import { generateResumeSummaryWithOpenAI } from "../providers/openAIResumeSummaryProvider.js";

/*
 * =========================================
 * Available AI Providers
 * =========================================
 */

const AI_PROVIDERS = {
  groq: generateResumeSummaryWithGroq,
  openai: generateResumeSummaryWithOpenAI,
};

/*
 * =========================================
 * Provider Selection
 * =========================================
 */

function getAIProviderName() {
  return String(process.env.AI_PROVIDER || "groq")
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Generate Resume Summary
 * =========================================
 */

export async function generateResumeSummary(context = {}) {
  const providerName = getAIProviderName();

  const generateWithProvider = AI_PROVIDERS[providerName];

  if (!generateWithProvider) {
    const error = new Error(`Unsupported AI_PROVIDER "${providerName}".`);

    error.status = 500;
    error.publicMessage =
      'The configured AI provider is invalid. Use "groq" or "openai".';

    throw error;
  }

  return generateWithProvider(context);
}

FILE: D:\Documents\Project\Portfolio\portfolio-plateform\backend\src\services\Service\sessionService.js

import {
  createSession as createSessionRepository,
  findActiveSessionByRefreshTokenHash,
  listActiveUserSessions,
  revokeAllUserSessions,
  revokeSession,
  revokeSessionByRefreshTokenHash,
  rotateSessionRefreshToken,
} from "../../repositories/sessionRepository.js";

import {
  createOpaqueTokenRecord,
  hashToken,
} from "../../security/tokenSecurity.js";

import {
  getRequestIpHash,
  getRequestUserAgent,
} from "../../security/requestSecurity.js";

import { getRefreshTokenExpiration } from "../../security/sessionCookieSecurity.js";

/*
 * =========================================
 * Session Token Purpose
 * =========================================
 */

const SESSION_REFRESH_TOKEN_PURPOSE = "session-refresh";

/*
 * =========================================
 * Session Errors
 * =========================================
 */

function createSessionError({ message, code, status = 401, publicMessage }) {
  const error = new Error(message);

  error.name = "SessionError";

  error.code = code;

  error.status = status;

  error.publicMessage = publicMessage;

  return error;
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * API Client Detection
 * =========================================
 */

function detectApiClient(userAgent) {
  if (/PostmanRuntime/i.test(userAgent)) {
    return "Postman API Client";
  }

  if (/Insomnia/i.test(userAgent)) {
    return "Insomnia API Client";
  }

  if (/HTTPie/i.test(userAgent)) {
    return "HTTPie Client";
  }

  if (/curl/i.test(userAgent)) {
    return "cURL Client";
  }

  if (/Wget/i.test(userAgent)) {
    return "Wget Client";
  }

  return "";
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Browser Detection
 * =========================================
 */

function detectBrowser(userAgent) {
  /*
   * Edge must be checked before Chrome
   * because Edge user agents also contain
   * the word "Chrome".
   */

  if (/Edg\//i.test(userAgent)) {
    return "Edge";
  }

  /*
   * Opera must also be checked before
   * Chrome because Chromium-based Opera
   * user agents contain "Chrome".
   */

  if (/OPR\//i.test(userAgent) || /Opera\//i.test(userAgent)) {
    return "Opera";
  }

  if (/Chrome\//i.test(userAgent) || /CriOS\//i.test(userAgent)) {
    return "Chrome";
  }

  if (/Firefox\//i.test(userAgent) || /FxiOS\//i.test(userAgent)) {
    return "Firefox";
  }

  if (
    /Safari\//i.test(userAgent) &&
    !/Chrome\//i.test(userAgent) &&
    !/CriOS\//i.test(userAgent) &&
    !/Chromium\//i.test(userAgent)
  ) {
    return "Safari";
  }

  return "Browser";
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Operating-System Detection
 * =========================================
 */

function detectOperatingSystem(userAgent) {
  /*
   * Check iOS before macOS because some
   * Apple user agents contain overlapping
   * platform information.
   */

  if (/iPhone|iPad|iPod/i.test(userAgent)) {
    return "iOS";
  }

  if (/Android/i.test(userAgent)) {
    return "Android";
  }

  if (/Windows Phone/i.test(userAgent)) {
    return "Windows Phone";
  }

  if (/Windows/i.test(userAgent)) {
    return "Windows";
  }

  if (/CrOS/i.test(userAgent)) {
    return "ChromeOS";
  }

  if (/Macintosh|Mac OS X/i.test(userAgent)) {
    return "macOS";
  }

  if (/Linux/i.test(userAgent)) {
    return "Linux";
  }

  return "Unknown device";
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Device-Name Detection
 * =========================================
 */

export function detectDeviceName(userAgent) {
  const value = typeof userAgent === "string" ? userAgent.trim() : "";

  if (!value) {
    return "Unknown device";
  }

  /*
   * API clients do not normally provide a
   * browser-style operating-system section.
   * Detect them before browser detection.
   */

  const apiClient = detectApiClient(value);

  if (apiClient) {
    return apiClient.slice(0, 200);
  }

  const browser = detectBrowser(value);

  const operatingSystem = detectOperatingSystem(value);

  return `${browser} on ${operatingSystem}`.slice(0, 200);
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Public Session Mapping
 * =========================================
 */

export function createPublicSession(session, currentSessionId = null) {
  if (!session) {
    return null;
  }

  return {
    id: session.id,

    deviceName: session.deviceName || "Unknown device",

    createdAt: session.createdAt,

    lastUsedAt: session.lastUsedAt,

    expiresAt: session.expiresAt,

    isCurrent: Boolean(currentSessionId && session.id === currentSessionId),
  };
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Create Authenticated Session
 * =========================================
 */

export async function createAuthenticatedSession({ userId, request }) {
  const refreshToken = createOpaqueTokenRecord(SESSION_REFRESH_TOKEN_PURPOSE);

  const userAgent = getRequestUserAgent(request);

  const ipAddressHash = getRequestIpHash(request);

  const expiresAt = getRefreshTokenExpiration();

  const session = await createSessionRepository({
    userId,

    refreshTokenHash: refreshToken.hash,

    deviceName: detectDeviceName(userAgent),

    userAgent,

    ipAddressHash,

    expiresAt,
  });

  return {
    refreshToken: refreshToken.token,

    refreshTokenExpiresAt: session.expiresAt,

    session: createPublicSession(session, session.id),
  };
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Refresh Authenticated Session
 * =========================================
 *
 * Every successful refresh rotates the
 * token. The previous token immediately
 * stops working.
 */

export async function refreshAuthenticatedSession({ refreshToken, request }) {
  if (typeof refreshToken !== "string" || refreshToken.length < 32) {
    throw createSessionError({
      message: "The refresh token is missing or malformed.",

      code: "SESSION_REFRESH_TOKEN_INVALID",

      publicMessage: "Your session is invalid or expired. Sign in again.",
    });
  }

  const currentTokenHash = hashToken(
    refreshToken,
    SESSION_REFRESH_TOKEN_PURPOSE,
  );

  const existing = await findActiveSessionByRefreshTokenHash(currentTokenHash);

  if (!existing) {
    throw createSessionError({
      message: "No active session matches the refresh token.",

      code: "SESSION_NOT_FOUND",

      publicMessage: "Your session is invalid or expired. Sign in again.",
    });
  }

  const nextRefreshToken = createOpaqueTokenRecord(
    SESSION_REFRESH_TOKEN_PURPOSE,
  );

  const userAgent = getRequestUserAgent(request);

  const expiresAt = getRefreshTokenExpiration();

  const rotatedSession = await rotateSessionRefreshToken({
    sessionId: existing.session.id,

    currentRefreshTokenHash: currentTokenHash,

    newRefreshTokenHash: nextRefreshToken.hash,

    newExpiresAt: expiresAt,

    ipAddressHash: getRequestIpHash(request),

    userAgent,
  });

  if (!rotatedSession) {
    throw createSessionError({
      message: "The session refresh token was already rotated or revoked.",

      code: "SESSION_REFRESH_CONFLICT",

      status: 409,

      publicMessage: "Your session changed in another request. Sign in again.",
    });
  }

  return {
    refreshToken: nextRefreshToken.token,

    refreshTokenExpiresAt: rotatedSession.expiresAt,

    session: createPublicSession(rotatedSession, rotatedSession.id),

    user: existing.user,

    roles: existing.roles,
  };
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Get Authenticated Session
 * =========================================
 *
 * Resolves the current user and session from
 * the refresh-token cookie without rotating
 * the refresh token.
 */

export async function getAuthenticatedSession({ refreshToken }) {
  if (typeof refreshToken !== "string" || refreshToken.length < 32) {
    throw createSessionError({
      message: "The refresh token is missing or malformed.",

      code: "SESSION_REFRESH_TOKEN_INVALID",

      publicMessage: "Your session is invalid or expired. Sign in again.",
    });
  }

  const refreshTokenHash = hashToken(
    refreshToken,
    SESSION_REFRESH_TOKEN_PURPOSE,
  );

  const existing = await findActiveSessionByRefreshTokenHash(refreshTokenHash);

  if (!existing) {
    throw createSessionError({
      message: "No active session matches the refresh token.",

      code: "SESSION_NOT_FOUND",

      publicMessage: "Your session is invalid or expired. Sign in again.",
    });
  }

  return {
    user: existing.user,

    roles: existing.roles,

    session: createPublicSession(existing.session, existing.session.id),
  };
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * List User Sessions
 * =========================================
 */

export async function getUserSessions({ userId, currentSessionId = null }) {
  const sessions = await listActiveUserSessions(userId);

  return sessions.map((session) =>
    createPublicSession(session, currentSessionId),
  );
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Revoke One User Session
 * =========================================
 */

export async function revokeUserSession({ sessionId, userId }) {
  const revokedSession = await revokeSession({
    sessionId,

    userId,

    reason: "user_revoked",
  });

  if (!revokedSession) {
    throw createSessionError({
      message: "The requested session does not exist or is already revoked.",

      code: "SESSION_NOT_FOUND",

      status: 404,

      publicMessage: "The requested session was not found.",
    });
  }

  return {
    sessionId: revokedSession.id,

    revokedAt: revokedSession.revokedAt,
  };
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Revoke Current Session
 * =========================================
 */

export async function revokeCurrentSession({
  refreshToken,
  reason = "logout",
}) {
  if (typeof refreshToken !== "string" || !refreshToken) {
    return {
      revoked: false,
    };
  }

  const refreshTokenHash = hashToken(
    refreshToken,
    SESSION_REFRESH_TOKEN_PURPOSE,
  );

  const revokedSession = await revokeSessionByRefreshTokenHash({
    refreshTokenHash,

    reason,
  });

  return {
    revoked: Boolean(revokedSession),

    sessionId: revokedSession?.id || null,
  };
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Revoke All User Sessions
 * =========================================
 */

export async function revokeEveryUserSession({
  userId,
  exceptSessionId = null,
  reason = "all_sessions_revoked",
}) {
  const normalizedReason =
    String(reason).trim().slice(0, 200) || "all_sessions_revoked";

  const revokedCount = await revokeAllUserSessions({
    userId,

    exceptSessionId,

    reason: normalizedReason,
  });

  return {
    revokedCount,
  };
}

import crypto from "node:crypto";
import fs from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";

import { getEnvironment } from "../../config/environment.js";

const environment = getEnvironment();

/*
 * =========================================
 * User Storage Identity
 * =========================================
 */

export function getProfilePictureUserDirectory(userId) {
  const normalizedUserId = String(userId || "").trim();

  if (!normalizedUserId) {
    throw new Error(
      "An authenticated user ID is required for profile-picture storage.",
    );
  }

  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(normalizedUserId).digest("hex");
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Storage Root
 * =========================================
 */

export function getProfilePictureStorageRoot() {
  const storageRoot = String(
    environment.PROFILE_PICTURE_STORAGE_PATH || "",
  ).trim();

  if (!storageRoot) {
    throw new Error("PROFILE_PICTURE_STORAGE_PATH is not configured.");
  }

  return path.resolve(storageRoot);
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * User Storage Path
 * =========================================
 */

export function getProfilePictureUserStoragePath(userId) {
  return path.join(
    getProfilePictureStorageRoot(),
    getProfilePictureUserDirectory(userId),
  );
}

/*
 * =========================================
 * Storage Key
 * =========================================
 */

export function createProfilePictureStorageKey(userId, fileName) {
  const safeFileName = path.basename(String(fileName || "").trim());

  if (!safeFileName) {
    throw new Error("A profile-picture filename is required.");
  }

  return [getProfilePictureUserDirectory(userId), safeFileName].join("/");
}

export function resolveOwnedProfilePicturePath(userId, storageKey) {
  const normalizedStorageKey = String(storageKey || "")
    .trim()
    .replaceAll("\\", "/");

  if (!normalizedStorageKey) {
    throw new Error("A profile-picture storage key is required.");
  }

  const userDirectory = getProfilePictureUserDirectory(userId);

  const expectedPrefix = `${userDirectory}/`;

  if (!normalizedStorageKey.startsWith(expectedPrefix)) {
    throw new Error(
      "The profile-picture storage key does not belong to the authenticated user.",
    );
  }

  const relativeFileName = normalizedStorageKey.slice(expectedPrefix.length);

  /*
   * A profile picture must be one file directly inside
   * the authenticated user's storage directory.
   */

  if (
    !relativeFileName ||
    relativeFileName.includes("/") ||
    relativeFileName === "." ||
    relativeFileName === ".." ||
    path.basename(relativeFileName) !== relativeFileName
  ) {
    throw new Error("The profile-picture storage key is invalid.");
  }

  const userStoragePath = path.resolve(
    getProfilePictureUserStoragePath(userId),
  );

  const resolvedFilePath = path.resolve(userStoragePath, relativeFileName);

  /*
   * Defense in depth:
   * even after validating the storage key above, verify that
   * the resulting file remains directly inside this user's
   * storage directory.
   */

  if (path.dirname(resolvedFilePath) !== userStoragePath) {
    throw new Error(
      "The profile-picture path is outside the authenticated user's storage directory.",
    );
  }

  return resolvedFilePath;
}

export async function deleteOwnedProfilePicture(userId, storageKey) {
  const filePath = resolveOwnedProfilePicturePath(userId, storageKey);

  try {
    await fs.unlink(filePath);

    return {
      deleted: true,
      storageKey,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    /*
     * If the file is already gone, cleanup has still reached
     * the desired state. Treat deletion as idempotent.
     */

    if (error?.code === "ENOENT") {
      return {
        deleted: false,
        storageKey,
      };
    }

    throw error;
  }
}

import crypto from "node:crypto";
import fs from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";

import { getEnvironment } from "../../config/environment.js";

function requireIdentifier(value, label) {
  const normalized = String(value ?? "").trim();

  if (!normalized) {
    throw new Error(`${label} is required for Project media storage.`);
  }

  return normalized;
}

function hashIdentifier(value) {
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(value).digest("hex");
}

function validateFileName(fileName) {
  const normalized = String(fileName ?? "").trim();

  if (
    !normalized ||
    normalized === "." ||
    normalized === ".." ||
    normalized.includes("/") ||
    normalized.includes("\\") ||
    path.basename(normalized) !== normalized ||
    !/^[a-f0-9-]{36}\.(?:jpg|jpeg|png|webp|avif|gif|mp4|webm)$/i.test(
      normalized,
    )
  ) {
    throw new Error("Invalid Project media filename.");
  }

  return normalized;
}

export function getProjectMediaStorageRoot() {
  const environment = getEnvironment();

  const configuredPath = String(
    environment.PROJECT_MEDIA_STORAGE_PATH || "",
  ).trim();

  if (!configuredPath) {
    throw new Error("PROJECT_MEDIA_STORAGE_PATH is not configured.");
  }

  return path.resolve(configuredPath);
}

export function getProjectMediaUserDirectory(userId) {
  return hashIdentifier(requireIdentifier(userId, "User ID"));
}

export function getProjectMediaProjectDirectory(projectId) {
  return hashIdentifier(requireIdentifier(projectId, "Project ID"));
}

export function getProjectMediaDirectory(userId, projectId) {
  return path.join(
    getProjectMediaStorageRoot(),
    getProjectMediaUserDirectory(userId),
    getProjectMediaProjectDirectory(projectId),
  );
}

export function createProjectMediaStorageKey(userId, projectId, fileName) {
  const safeFileName = validateFileName(fileName);

  return [
    getProjectMediaUserDirectory(userId),
    getProjectMediaProjectDirectory(projectId),
    safeFileName,
  ].join("/");
}

export function resolveOwnedProjectMediaPath(userId, projectId, storageKey) {
  const normalizedKey = String(storageKey ?? "").trim();

  const expectedUserDirectory = getProjectMediaUserDirectory(userId);
  const expectedProjectDirectory = getProjectMediaProjectDirectory(projectId);

  const parts = normalizedKey.split("/");

  if (
    parts.length !== 3 ||
    parts[0] !== expectedUserDirectory ||
    parts[1] !== expectedProjectDirectory
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Project media storage key does not belong to this user and Project.",
    );
  }

  const safeFileName = validateFileName(parts[2]);

  const mediaDirectory = path.resolve(
    getProjectMediaDirectory(userId, projectId),
  );

  const filePath = path.resolve(mediaDirectory, safeFileName);

  if (path.dirname(filePath) !== mediaDirectory) {
    throw new Error("Project media path is outside the expected directory.");
  }

  return filePath;
}

export async function assertSafeProjectMediaDirectory(userId, projectId) {
  const root = getProjectMediaStorageRoot();
  const userDirectory = path.join(root, getProjectMediaUserDirectory(userId));
  const projectDirectory = path.join(
    userDirectory,
    getProjectMediaProjectDirectory(projectId),
  );

  const directories = [root, userDirectory, projectDirectory];

  for (const directory of directories) {
    const stats = await fs.lstat(directory);

    if (stats.isSymbolicLink() || !stats.isDirectory()) {
      throw new Error(
        "Project media storage directory is not a regular directory.",
      );
    }
  }

  const canonicalRoot = await fs.realpath(root);
  const canonicalProjectDirectory = await fs.realpath(projectDirectory);

  const relative = path.relative(
    canonicalRoot,
    canonicalProjectDirectory,
  );

  if (
    relative === "" ||
    relative === ".." ||
    relative.startsWith(`..${path.sep}`) ||
    path.isAbsolute(relative)
  ) {
    throw new Error("Project media directory is outside the storage root.");
  }
}

export async function deleteOwnedProjectMedia(userId, projectId, storageKey) {
  const filePath = resolveOwnedProjectMediaPath(userId, projectId, storageKey);

  try {
    await assertSafeProjectMediaDirectory(userId, projectId);

    const stats = await fs.lstat(filePath);

    if (stats.isSymbolicLink() || !stats.isFile()) {
      throw new Error("Project media target is not a regular file.");
    }

    await fs.unlink(filePath);

    return {
      deleted: true,
      storageKey,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    if (error?.code === "ENOENT") {
      return {
        deleted: false,
        storageKey,
      };
    }

    throw error;
  }
}
